# 20 - Model tiers by GPU size, benchmarks, and fine-tuning (research checkpoint)

> **Status - 2026-10-03 [A].** A **checkpoint dump** of agent-reported research, written to disk so
> the context is not lost. It has not been audited. External facts (HuggingFace model sizes,
> licences, llama.cpp issues, benchmark numbers) are [E], fetched by agents on 2026-10-03; 72 of 74
> candidate claim-sets needed correction by their verifier, so **the sizes below are the verified
> ones** and the scouts' raw lists are not reproduced. Repo `path:line` citations were not
> re-verified by the author of this page. **Nothing here is a claim about accuracy on our task:**
> no candidate except Qwen3-VL-8B has a `tierb-v0` number. A curated, audited version will supersede
> this page. Inputs: a 101-agent workflow (6 tier scouts, 9 family scouts, 74 per-model verifiers,
> 3 research tracks, a size-versus-accuracy analysis, a completeness critic).

## 1. The owner's questions and decisions (2026-10-03)

Questions: (1) we use a generic VLM - do we need a simple security benchmark (possibly with Harbor)
or an existing security benchmark, then LoRA fine-tuning or training our own? (2) Which HuggingFace
VLMs for roughly 10, 12, 16, 24 and 48 GB NVIDIA RTX cards, possibly a different model per tier?
(3) With more system resources, should scoring be more accurate?

Owner answers to the questions the assistant asked (2026-10-03) [O]:

- Run the two free S3 experiments (rubric prompt, logprob score) on the GB300 now.
- Decide whether S3 >= 90% is attainable from a single still **after** those experiments.
- Move the shipped assess call from temperature 0.1 (unseeded) to temperature 0, as a small code
  change on this branch.
- Keep running replays with the renderer-stopped check bypassed (the VLM is in a fenced
  `agent-gpu` container), and label every such run.

## 2. Does more compute buy accuracy? (size-versus-accuracy analysis)

**Answer.** Only partly, and on our own data VRAM is the weakest of the levers for the S3 gap. [V][C] I recomputed S2/S3 from the eval store (read-only; runs e3fd…, c79e…, 42e0…). The temperature-0 replay gives S3 88/241 = 36.5% and S2 18/209 = 8.6%, the same as the T=0.1 runs, so sampling noise is not the gap. [C] Of the 153 S3 misses, 148 (97%) have verdict=confirmed, 127 (83%) are scored 10 or below, and only 12 (8%) are within 15 points of their floor. About 71% of miss texts argue non-threat (calm, relaxed, delivering); 0 of the 88 hits do (keyword heuristic). So the 8B sees and describes the declared object, then scores risk as "observed aggression", while the corpus bands treat a visible weapon or an unattended child as high or critical. The shipped prompt asks for "risk_score 0-100" with no rubric (backend/services/vlm_client.py:537-554) [V]. [C] The scores are polarized: 68% sit in {0,5,10}, 23% are 75 or above, and 8% fall between 20 and 70. AUROC for incident vs benign is 0.70, and the best single threshold reaches only 42% TPR at 5% FPR (52% on threats only). Recalibrating or thresholding the emitted score therefore cannot reach 90%. Averaging three replays only slides along the same curve: mean-of-3 gives S3 32.5%/S2 8.1%, max 41.7%/12.9%, min 30.8%/4.8%. [C] Splitting the 241 incidents by the scenes' evidence type: (A) 64 stranger/intent scenes (package_theft, loitering, peering, trying_car_doors, casing, tailgating), where the 8B gets 2/64, the flagship about 7/64, and twin-pair AUROCs are 0.28-0.58; (B) 82 context-risk scenes (child_alone_at_pool, blunt_weapon, person_down, vandalism and others), 18/82 for the 8B vs about 58/82 for the flagship; (C) 95 object-cued scenes (firearm, knife, fire, masked, forced entry), 68/95 vs about 77/95. About 41 of the flagship's +54 hits are in B, only about +5 in A, and C is nearly flat. Even with B and C perfect, S3 caps at 177/241 = 73% unless at least 40 of the 64 A scenes become recoverable, which is a corpus-construct limit that no VRAM changes. [E] The published evidence agrees that size is not the main driver: Qwen3-VL-32B scores below the 8B on TAR-Bench (28.0 vs 30.9) while Qwen3.5-9B scores 39.1 (arXiv 2608.10317), and open models show perception without reasoning on safety benchmarks (EgoSafe 2607.26518). Real VRAM tiers [C]: the measured A5500 stack of 9.38-9.60 GiB becomes about 12.8-13.0 GiB with the 8B at Q8_0, 20.3-20.6 GiB at F16 (about the 24 GB bar), about 25-25.5 GiB for 32B Q4_K_M (32 GB tier), and about 39-39.5 GiB for 32B Q8_0 (48 GB tier). Spend VRAM last. Run the free experiments first: a rubric prompt, then a logprob-based score, then a 2x2 of prompt by model size. Caveats: tierb-v0 uses one FLUX still per event, an ideal detector and declared labels, so all of this is optimistic and the synthetic-to-real gap is unmeasured [?]. The external studies cover VQA, traffic CCTV and forensics, not home-security risk scoring [?].

**Which regime are we in.** Mainly calibration (decision policy), then data/construct, with capability third. Resolution and quantization are not implicated by any evidence we have, but they are also untested here. [C] Calibration: 148 of 153 S3 misses are verdict=confirmed (r1 149/154, r2 151/156). 127 of 153 (83%) are scored 10 or below, with a median 55 points under the floor. About 71% of miss texts argue non-threat (a knife "held loosely" scored 10, a baseball bat "in batting stance" scored 0, a child "calm at pool edge" scored 10). The shipped prompt gives no rubric (vlm_client.py:537-554 [V]). The flagship, which is far more capable, still lands at S3 58.9% with a "verifies detections, risk follows conservatively" pattern (p5a-2026-09-30.md:26-28 [V]); 41.9% of its incident scores are below band. Instruction-tuned verbalized scores are also known to polarize (folktexts, 2407.14614 [E]), and ours are polarized: 68% in {0,5,10}, 23% at 75 or above, 8% in between. [C] Data/construct: package_theft, loitering, peering, trying_car_doors, casing and tailgating together are 64 of 241 incidents (26.6%). The 8B hits 2/64 and the flagship about 7/64; twin-pair AUROC against the benign look-alike is 0.28-0.58 (package vs delivery_driver 0.58, loitering vs neighbor_passing 0.28; n=9-29, so ±0.1-0.2). Even a perfect B+C leaves S3 ≤ 177/241 = 73% unless these become recoverable from a single still. The oracle best-of-both-models bound is S3 ≤ 60.6-66.4%, because 95 of 450 items are wrong for both the 8B and the flagship (derived from the committed counts 167/117 errors, 72/22 exclusive). Label noise from generation is small: the audit found scene/people/conditions error 0.0% [0-6.0] and prop missing 1 of 15 (6.7%) [V p5a:74-85]. [C] Capability: the flagship's gain is real but concentrated in B (+41 of +54 hits; child_alone_at_pool 0 to about 14/19; minor threats 17 to about 44 of 63). It does not reduce false alarms (S2 6.7 to 8.6%; flashlight_neighbor 3.4 to 24.1%). [?] Resolution: the 8B correctly describes the declared props in nearly all misses, so perception is unlikely to be binding. The "confirmed" verdict is weak evidence of seeing, because the ideal detector hands the model the class (all 29 machete items score 10 or below, all 19 knife items are median 85), and a swapped-detection ablation has not been run. Sampling noise (sd of about 2.6 S2 items and 2.1 S3 items at T=0.1, [V] handoff:249-278) is the same size as most single-lever effects, so every arm needs T=0 and paired comparison.

### Levers

- **Bigger model, same prompt (32B Q4_K_M / flagship class)** (needs more VRAM: yes)
  - Expected effect: The measured jump from the 8B to the flagship is S3 36.5 to 58.9% (+54 hits) with S2 6.7 to 8.6%, and about 41 of those hits are in context-risk scenes (B). The result is still well under 90%, and it does not fix A. Expect a partial, family-dependent gain: Qwen3-VL-32B could land between the 8B and the flagship, or even below the 8B, as on TAR-Bench. VRAM [C]: 32B Q4_K_M is about 25.0-25.5 GiB (fits 32 GB, not 24 GB); 32B Q8_0 is about 39-39.5 GiB (fits 48 GB by 1.3-1.8 GiB).
  - Evidence: [V] p5a-2026-09-30.md:19-31,62-65. [C] hits derived from the per-scenario table (rounded percentages). [E] Qwen/Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct-GGUF: Q4_K_M 19,762,150,432 B, Q8_0 34,817,720,352 B, mmproj Q8_0 772,360,224 B. [E] config: 64 layers x 8 KV heads x 128, so KV about 4.25 GiB at q8_0 for 2x16K [C]. [E] TAR-Bench (2608.10317): Qwen3-VL-32B mean 28.0 vs 8B 30.9, although it is higher on closed-form QA (BCQ 65.0 vs 59.6, MCQ 71.2 vs 62.9). [E] Qwen3-VL report 2511.21631: 2B to 8B (thinking) MMStar 68.1 to 75.3.
- **Higher-precision weights (8B Q8_0 or F16, mmproj F16)** (needs more VRAM: yes)
  - Expected effect: Likely small, with no systematic lift: a few S3/S2 item flips (borderline items flip) but not the 50-point gap. Cost is +3.43 GiB for Q8_0 (stack about 12.8-13.0 GiB; fits 16 GB, not 12 GB). F16 plus F16 mmproj is +10.96 GiB (about 20.3-20.6 GiB, which is the 24 GB bar, so a 32 GB card).
  - Evidence: [E] Qwen3VL-8B-Instruct-GGUF: F16 16,388,044,896 B, Q4_K_M 5,027,784,800 B, Q8_0 8,709,519,456 B, mmproj F16 1,159,029,824 B vs Q8_0 752,289,728 B. [E] llama.cpp quantize README: Q4_K_M 4.8944 bpw, Q8_0 8.5008, F16 16.0005 (Llama-3.1-8B). [E] 2601.15287: GPTQ/AWQ preserve full-precision VQA/captioning at 3.5-4.5 bpw on BLIP-2/LLaVA-1.5-7B; the LLM is the most sensitive component, and the ViT keeps 20-30% importance under GPTQ despite 4.3% of parameters (we already keep the mmproj at Q8_0). [E] 2607.08734 and 2405.00632: quantization can shift decision-level behavior and confidence on borderline items even when accuracy is preserved. [E] Qwen FP8 card: 'nearly identical to BF16' (vendor claim). [?] No VLM K-quant study on safety/surveillance found. [C] Stack = weights + mmproj + KV 2.39 GiB + 1.61-1.82 GiB overhead (measured 9,606/9,826 MiB, ledger item 39-40).
- **More image tokens per still (cap 1280 to about 2040 native)** (needs more VRAM: no)
  - Expected effect: Probably small. The cap is about 63% of native tokens (about a 20% linear downscale of a 1920x1080 still) [C]. The 8B already describes the declared knife, bat and fire in most misses; the cost is prefill latency (S4), not VRAM.
  - Evidence: [V] ledger item 40 (line 407-408): uncapped 4-still stack 9,826 MiB vs capped 9,722 MiB (different prompt sizes), so about +0.1 GiB. [V] vlm_client.py:107 (\_IMAGE_TOKENS_PER_FRAME = 1280). [E] config: patch 16, merge 2, so 32 px per token; 1920x1080 is about 60x34 = 2,040 tokens [C]. [E] VisionThink 2507.13348: general VQA mostly holds at 1/4 resolution, with drops mainly on OCR. [E] Qwen3-VL report: zoom/tool gains of about 5 pts on V\* outweigh size gains for fine-grained perception (not our regime).
- **More frames per call (up to 4 stills; sequences)** (needs more VRAM: no)
  - Expected effect: This is the only lever that adds information for the A scenes (stranger vs resident, taking vs delivering). 4 x 1280 = 5,120 tokens fits the 16,384-token slot at the shipped 2x16K pool (no extra VRAM; cost is latency). Uncertain gain: published video-LLMs often do not convert frames into motion/intent understanding. tierb-v0 replays send 1 still, and only 17 incident clips are 'ready' (205 still 'prompted'), so S3 cannot be tested at scale yet.
  - Evidence: [V] export has still.jpg only per set; clip-index final state: incident ready 17, rendered 10, prompted 205, failed 9; benign ready 144 (/synthbench/corpus/tierb-v0/clip-index.jsonl). [E] MotionBlind 2609.09528: neither more frames nor smarter selection closes the motion-understanding gap, and scale does not help. [E] SurveillanceVQA-589K 2505.12589: all eight LVLMs (0.5B-7B) score lower on abnormal than normal clips. [E] Qwen3-VL report: video evals capped at 640-768 tokens/frame.
- **Thinking / reasoning mode (Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking, same weights size)** (needs more VRAM: no)
  - Expected effect: Uncertain, with high S4/S5 risk. It costs no weight VRAM (Thinking Q4_K_M is 5,027,784,704 B, essentially the Instruct 5,027,784,800 B) but needs 4k or more output tokens and a larger read timeout. A 1024-token budget would blow S5, and reasoning can lengthen p95 well past 30 s [C].
  - Evidence: [V] p5a-probes.md:86-110: the flagship with thinking spent the whole 1024-token budget with empty content; Cosmos looped (142 sentences) and refused about 13% on the tierb-v0 run at 4096 tokens. [E] VAU-R1 2505.23504: base models often do worse with CoT (hallucination). [E] TAR 2608.10317: CoT inference adds +0.5 after SFT (53.9 to 54.4). [E] 2505.20236: reasoning ('thinking with images') models show better verbalized calibration, but on QA, not risk scoring.
- **Second-opinion cascade (second model, resident or swapped)** (needs more VRAM: yes)
  - Expected effect: Capped by co-failure: for the 8B plus the flagship, both are wrong on 95/450 items, so the oracle S3 ceiling is 60.6-66.4% and a realistic router is lower. It helps S2 only if the second model disagrees on hard negatives, and the flagship did not (S2 8.6%). Latency is also paid on the escalated fraction.
  - Evidence: [C] both-wrong = 167 - 72 = 117 - 22 = 95 from the committed counts (p5a:343, S3 miss 153/99, S2 FP 14/18). [E] Co-failure ceiling 2606.27288: policy accuracy is at most 1 - beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong. [E] 2609.07403 (CNN gate to Qwen3-VL-8B on UCF-Crime): routing buys compute and FNR trade-offs, not an accuracy lift. [E] 2511.07171: a 65.8M 3D CNN had better calibration (ROC AUC 92.59%) than VLMs for violence.
- **k-sample self-consistency (mean/min/max of repeated calls)** (needs more VRAM: no)
  - Expected effect: No accuracy gain, only a trade along the same ROC, at k times the latency (S4). From the three existing replays: mean-of-3 S3 32.5%/S2 8.1%; max 41.7%/12.9%; min 30.8%/4.8%. Min-of-3 reaches the 5% S2 bar but costs S3 and 3x calls.
  - Evidence: [C] computed on the 449 items scored in all three runs (r1, r2, T0). The variance is real [V] handoff:259-263 (only 62% item-level identical across two runs), but T=0.1 sampling is not the bias.
- **Rubric/score-semantics prompt (severity anchors from the product's own bands; harm-if-as-it-appears wording)** (needs more VRAM: no)
  - Expected effect: The largest expected gain for the least cost, concentrated in groups B and C (about 64 + 27 misses). Expected ceiling about 73% S3 unless A is recoverable. Raises hard-negative false alarms (hooded_jogger, power_tools), so S2 must be watched.
  - Evidence: [V] the shipped prompt has no rubric (vlm_client.py:537-554). [V] ledger item 40 (:396-403): input-format changes moved the 8B's S3 from 5/20 to 13/20 on the 38-item set, with runs differing by about 3/20. [C] 97% of misses are 'confirmed' and 83% are scored 10 or below. [E] 2510.23190: few-shot prompting raises accuracy but raises false positives (an operating-point shift). [E] 2407.14614: instruction-tuned models polarize verbalized probabilities.
- **Logprob/logit score instead of verbalized integer** (needs more VRAM: no)
  - Expected effect: Recovers ranking information that the polarized integer hides. If AUROC rises from 0.70 to 0.85 or more, calibration headroom exists; if it stays at or below 0.72, it does not. Needs llama-server logprobs under json_schema grammar, which is unverified here [?].
  - Evidence: [C] 0.70 AUROC and 8% mid-range scores. [E] 2609.07455 (Qwen3-VL-2B / InternVL3.5-2B): a next-token Yes/No logit interface beat text-generation verification on frame-level AUC and AP, and the text interface was sensitive to prompts, parsers and model choice. [E] MAD-Guard 2609.33683 (Qwen3-VL-8B + LoRA r=16): a binary direct head halved ECE (0.0845 to 0.0450) for a -1.8 pt accuracy trade on a forensic image task (transfer [?]).
- **Newer-generation model at the same VRAM (Qwen3.5-9B class)** (needs more VRAM: no)
  - Expected effect: May beat larger older models: on TAR-Bench Qwen3.5-9B scores 39.1 vs Qwen3-VL-32B 28.0 and Qwen3-VL-8B 30.9. It is not measured on our task. [C] Q4_K_M 5.29 GiB plus F16 mmproj 0.86 GiB is the same VRAM class as the 8B. It is a hybrid with 8 full-attention layers, 4 KV heads, 256 dim, so KV per token is about 32,768 B vs 147,456 B for the 8B (about 4.5x smaller). It thinks by default. [C] Shipped llama.cpp b7972 (published 2026-02-08) predates its release (repo created 2026-02-27), so it needs a newer build (as Nemotron/Mistral did).
  - Evidence: [E] HF Qwen/Qwen3.5-9B: created 2026-02-27, apache-2.0, 9,653,104,368 params, image-text-to-text. [E] unsloth/Qwen3.5-9B-GGUF: Q4_K_M 5,680,522,464 B; mmproj-F16 918,166,080 B. [E] config layer_types: 32 layers, 8 full attention. [E] GitHub release b7972 published 2026-02-08. [E] TAR-Bench table (2608.10317). [?] Model-card claim 'outperforms Qwen3-VL' is vendor text; llama.cpp support not verified.
- **LoRA/SFT fine-tune on the corpus (or a real-camera set)** (needs more VRAM: yes)
  - Expected effect: The only lever with large published gains on the same 8B, but they measure policy alignment to the training labels. Training (not inference) needs more VRAM: 8B bf16 weights alone are 17.5 GB [C], and QLoRA footprint is [?]. Risk here: 450 FLUX stills with one author's declared bands; SFT can overfit and generalize poorly out of domain; the A scenes may be unlearnable from a single still; and the synthetic-to-real gap is unmeasured [?].
  - Evidence: [E] TAR 2608.10317: Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct mean 30.9 to 53.9 after multi-task SFT (44,040 annotations; the CR2-8B recipe is a full fine-tune, 3 epochs, 8xA100). [E] VAU-R1 2505.23504: a 3B RFT model beats the 7B base on ECVA mIoU, and SFT 'memorizes' and generalizes poorly out of domain. [E] MAD-Guard 2609.33683: LoRA r=16, 2,400 samples on Qwen3-VL-8B reached 94.9% on a closed forensic task. [E] Federated LoRA LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B >90% on RWF-2000 (2511.07171). [V] Nothing in the repo mentions VLM fine-tuning.
- **Longer context / more parallel slots** (needs more VRAM: yes)
  - Expected effect: Throughput and stream count only; no accuracy effect for single-still scoring. KV at q8_0 costs 78,336 B/token, so +16K per slot is about +1.2 GiB [C]; PARALLEL 2 to 4 at 16K is +2.39 GiB.
  - Evidence: [V] KV 2,448 MiB for 2x16384 at q8_0 (item 40); the 4B and 8B share 36 layers x 8 KV heads x 128 (confirmed from HF config [E]). [C] 2448 MiB / 32768 cells.
- **Fix the corpus construct (multi-frame sets for A scenes, relabel visually indeterminate scenarios, add real-camera stills)** (needs more VRAM: no)
  - Expected effect: Required to make the 90% S3 bar reachable at all: 64 of 241 incidents (26.6%) are not distinguishable from their benign twins in one still (8B hits 2/64, flagship about 7/64). No model or VRAM choice changes this.
  - Evidence: [C] A-group counts and twin AUROC (n=9-29, wide intervals; pairings are mine). [V] taxonomy package_theft [60,90] floor high vs delivery_driver [0,20] (tier_b_v0.yaml:304, benign delivery_driver at :139-147); loitering floor medium (:237). [?] Whether any model can separate them from one still is unmeasured beyond the flagship's 7/64.

### Experiments proposed (cheapest and most discriminating first)

- **E1 Rubric-only prompt arm (calibration test).** Reuse the existing T=0 shim used by 20261003T134900Z. Add severity anchors from the product's own bands (low 0-29, medium 30-59, high 60-84, critical 85-100) and a neutral 'risk = potential harm if the scene is as it appears, not only observed aggression' sentence, with no scenario names. Tune on batch-1/2 (about 150 events) and report on held-out batch-3/4/5. Compare paired against the T0 baseline (S3 88/241, S2 18/209) with McNemar on discordant items.
  - Cost: About 24 min GB300 per full replay (T0 run 13:49 to 14:13 UTC; p5a 18m43s); zero VRAM, no downloads. One or two prompt variants cost under an hour.
  - Discriminates: If the 8B's S3 rises sharply in groups B and C with S2 held near 5-9%, the gap is calibration (and the flagship's edge was mostly a better risk prior). If S3 stays near 36%, the policy is not prompt-reachable, which points to capability or fine-tuning. Either way A (64 scenes) should stay near 0-11%; if it moves, the construct is more recoverable than I estimated.
- **E2 Logprob-weighted score (ranking headroom).** Request token logprobs for the risk_score digits (first confirm llama-server b7972 returns them under json_schema grammar [?]). Compute the expected score and its AUROC for incident vs benign and threat vs hard-negative; compare with 0.70 / 0.74, and with the best TPR@5%FPR of 42%.
  - Cost: Small shim plus one T0 replay (about 24 min GB300); no VRAM or weights.
  - Discriminates: AUROC of 0.85 or more means the model holds ranking information that the polarized integer hides (calibration regime, fixable without VRAM). AUROC of 0.72 or less means the information is not in the model for these stills (capability/data regime).
- **E3 2x2: {shipped, rubric} x {Qwen3-VL-8B Q4_K_M, Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct Q4_K_M} at T=0.** Serve the 32B pair (19.76 GB + 0.77 GB mmproj, about 25 GiB [C]) on the GB300 under the same build (b7972 loads the qwen3vl arch, per the ledger). Replay both prompts on both models with identical max_tokens and the same 450 sets. The interaction is the point.
  - Cost: Download about 20.5 GB (owner-side, not done here) plus two 32B replays of about 30-40 min each [C]; the 8B arms reuse E1. Not runnable on a 24 GB card.
  - Discriminates: Whether the prompt effect shrinks with model size (policy mismatch) or whether size adds on top. If 8B/rubric is at or above 32B/shipped, buy the prompt before VRAM. If 32B/shipped clearly beats 8B/rubric on B, capability is real, justifying the 32 GB or 48 GB tier. TAR-Bench shows 32B can lose to 8B, so do not assume.
- **E4 Image-token cap sweep (640 / 1280 / 2048).** Change LLAMA_ARG_IMAGE_MAX_TOKENS (env) and the client's \_IMAGE_TOKENS_PER_FRAME pairing, restart ai-vlm, replay at T=0. 2048 is about native for 1920x1080 (about 2,040 tokens). Record llama-server peak VRAM to confirm the +0.1 GiB expectation.
  - Cost: Two new replays (640, 2048) of about 24 min each plus restarts; no download. Prefill rises about 1.6x at 2048, so also log S4.
  - Discriminates: If S3/S2 move by about 3 items or fewer between 1280 and 2048, resolution is not binding. If 640 loses nothing, the 1280 cap is above the knee and latency can be saved. If the knife/handgun scenes jump, perception at distance matters.
- **E5 Q8_0 vs Q4_K_M (plus mmproj F16) at T=0.** Official Qwen3VL-8B-Instruct-Q8_0.gguf (8,709,519,456 B) and optionally mmproj-F16 (1,159,029,824 B), same build/prompt, replay all 450, paired comparison; measure peak (expect about +3.43 GiB).
  - Cost: One replay (about 24 min) plus a 9.9 GB download (owner-side).
  - Discriminates: Precision. A few-item swing with no systematic direction rules quantization out and lets the 12/16 GB tiers keep Q4_K_M; a consistent gain on group B/C with fewer 0-10 scores would justify the 16 GB+ tier's Q8_0.
- **E6 Detection ablation/swap on a 60-item subset (does 'confirmed' mean 'saw it?').** Replay about 30 incident and 30 benign items with (a) the detection list withheld, (b) the declared prop swapped for an absent class (e.g., knife on a benign scene). Count rejected/uncertain vs confirmed and the scores.
  - Cost: About 3-5 min per arm; zero VRAM.
  - Discriminates: If swapped detections are still 'confirmed' at 90% or more, the 96-98% confirm rate is agreement with the detector, not perception, and resolution/capability could still hide inside it. It also shows how S2 would behave with real YOLO false positives, which the ideal detector never produces.
- **E7 Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking upper-bound arm.** Q4_K_M GGUF (5,027,784,704 B + mmproj Q8_0), max_tokens 4096, read_timeout 120 s (as the Cosmos shim). Replay a 150-item stratified subset first and record accuracy, tokens and latency.
  - Cost: About 5x decode tokens [C]: 150 items in about 40-60 min. It will fail S4/S5 as shipped, so this is an accuracy upper bound only.
  - Discriminates: Whether inference-time reasoning (rather than weights) closes group B/C. Published results are mixed (CoT hurts base models in VAU-R1; +0.5 after SFT in TAR). Skip it if E1 already closes B.
- **E8 Offline paired analysis on existing runs (already done, CPU only; extend as runs land).** Read-only SQL/Python over eval.sqlite: miss anatomy, score histogram, AUROC, twin AUROC, 3-run ensembles. Add the in-flight second T0 run (151/151 items bit-identical with the first at read time) to confirm T=0 determinism, and the flagship's raw rows (host eval store, not mounted here) to replace my percentage-derived group counts.
  - Cost: Minutes of CPU; no GPU or downloads.
  - Discriminates: Whether the A/B/C decomposition holds with exact flagship rows, and whether T=0 is truly deterministic (required for paired single-run comparisons).

## 3. Recommended model per GPU tier (fit and support only)

Budget per tier is 0.85 x card memory (spec S1). The shipped control is Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct Q4_K_M + mmproj Q8_0 under llama.cpp b7972. Totals are whole-stack estimates [C] from verified file sizes; buffer constants differ by track so cross-tier totals are comparable only to about +/-0.5 GiB.

### 10 GB card (budget 8.5 GiB)

- **Primary:** Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B (GGUF: unsloth/Qwen3.5-4B-GGUF) - Q8_0 (4,482,403,488 B) + mmproj-F16 (672,423,616 B); 2 slots x 16,384 ctx, q8_0 KV, --image-max-tokens 1280, thinking OFF [E sizes]. Step down to Q6_K (3,525,956,768 B) if the measured whole-stack peak exceeds ~8.0 GiB. - **7.31 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp newer than the shipped b7972 [E]. The qwen35 arch is absent at b7972 and b8000 and present at b8200 and b11090 (exact floor not bisected [?]). The repo already builds b11090 as ai-vlm:sm103-b11090, but that is sm_103 (GB300). An RTX 3080 needs a b11090-class rebuild with CUDA arch 86 (.env.example ships 89, VLM_REQUIRED_BUILD pins b7972). Extra work: rebuild; move the pin; re-run the S-2 json_schema enforcement probe with images; send enable_thinking=false or --reasoning off (the shipped client sends neither); the mmproj exists only as F16/BF16/F32, so there is no Q8_0 mmproj (+0.2 GiB; self-quantizing is unverified [?]); add a models.py row; re-run the Qwen3-VL-8B control on the same build. vLLM is NOT needed: the 4B AWQ (cyankiwi, 3.78 GiB safetensors [E]) would add a second engine, and Ampere has no FP8/FP4 tensor cores so NVFP4/FP8 buy nothing here.
  - Why: Best expected capability per GiB at 10 GB, ranked on vendor cards plus fit margin plus runtime risk. Nothing here has been run on tierb-v0 except Qwen3-VL. Vendor card [E, vendor-run, not our task]: MMMU 77.6, MMMU-Pro 66.3, MMStar 78.3, RealWorldQA 79.5, CountBench 96.3, VlmsAreBlind 92.6, HallusionBench 65.0. Against Qwen3-VL-4B (named on the Qwen3.5-2B card): MMStar 73.2, RealWorldQA 73.2, HallusionBench 64.1, i.e. +5 to +6 on general VQA but only +0.9 on HallusionBench, the nearest S2 proxy. It sits below Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B on HallusionBench (66.0). Hybrid KV: only 8 of 32 layers hold KV, so 32,768 B/token vs 147,456 for Qwen3-VL-4B/8B [C]. That leaves ~1.2 GiB spare at Q8_0, so spare VRAM buys weight precision, not slots: near-lossless weights matter because our S3 miss is calibration (62.7% of Qwen incident scores sit ~54 pts below band), not missed objects. Decode reads ~4.2 GiB/token, similar to the shipped 8B Q4_K_M at 4.7 GiB, so S4 should be in the same class [C, unmeasured]. 4B is also the best LoRA base at this tier (Unsloth guide linked from its GGUF card [E]; Qwen3.5 LoRA-to-GGUF conversion was only fixed 2026-09-30, #28324, after b11090, so merge before quantizing or use a newer build). No claim of S2/S3 accuracy is made here, only fit and support. Verifier notes: the unsloth GGUF's embedded template emits an empty think block unless enable_thinking is true [E], so thinking is probably off by default, but pin it explicitly. llama.cpp #20345 (grammar not enforced while thinking is on) is closed but the fix is unconfirmed [?]. #25717 (CUDA illegal access, FA on + q8_0 KV, Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, sm_120) was closed stale and unfixed; the 10 GB tier is sm_86 so it is less likely to apply. #29629 (CUDA FA gives wrong mmproj embeddings, grows with image size, reported on sm_86 A2000 and on Qwen3.5-4B, open) does apply and also touches the shipped Qwen3-VL path.
- **Fallback:** Qwen/Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct-GGUF - Q4_K_M (5,027,784,800 B) + mmproj Q8_0 (752,289,728 B), the shipped pair, run with --parallel 1 x 16,384 ctx, q8_0 KV (half the measured 2,448 MiB pool = 1,224 MiB) [E sizes] - **8.19 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp b7972 as shipped. No engine, client or prompt change; it is the control for every other row.
  - Why: This is the only candidate with real tierb-v0 numbers and an S5 pass (S3 36.1-36.5% FAIL, S2 6.7-9.1% marginal, S4 p95 16.2 s PASS on A5500, S5 0 refusals on the re-run, 1 length truncation in 450). It is also the only one with an identical engine and a proven json_schema path. Fit is razor-thin. Measured whole-stack peak on the A5500 at 2 slots is 9,606 MiB; subtracting one slot's KV (1,224 MiB) gives 8,382 MiB = 8.19 GiB [C]. The calibrated formula gives 8.46 GiB, so the margin to 8.5 is 0.04-0.31 GiB. Measured llama-server alone: 8,822 - 1,224 = 7,598 MiB = 7.42 GiB. It fits only at 1 slot (single-user product, so concurrent cameras serialize), and no 10 GB-class measurement exists [?]. A larger image-token cap would eat the margin. If the thin margin fails on a real card, the same-engine step-downs are the 8B IQ4_XS from the unsloth mirror (4.27 GiB; 8.05 GiB by formula; second provenance hop, needs the official Q8_0 mmproj) or Qwen3-VL-4B (measured 7.02-7.06 GiB). The 4B fails S5 as shipped (8/13 truncated at 1,024 tokens).
- **Ambitious:** Qwen/Qwen3.5-9B (unsloth/Qwen3.5-9B-GGUF), IQ4_XS (5,168,653,536 B) + mmproj-F16 (918,166,080 B), 2 slots x 16k, q8_0 KV, thinking off, on the same b11090-class sm_86 build. Whole stack is 8.18 GiB by the calibrated formula (margin 0.32 GiB) or 7.87 GiB on the verifiers' lower buffer basis [C]. The margin is inside our +/-0.3 GiB calibration error, so any buffer growth from a higher image-token cap or a third slot breaks S1. Q4_K_M at 2 slots is 8.66 GiB calibrated (8.34 on the lower basis), so it does not fit reliably; at 1 slot it is 8.35 GiB (margin 0.15). Vendor gains over the 4B are small: MMMU 78.4 vs 77.6, HallusionBench 69.3 vs 65.0, OCRBench 89.2 vs 85.0, LingoQA 80.4 vs 74.4 [E, vendor]. The price is a ~4.5-bit imatrix quant whose effect on calibration-sensitive scoring is unmeasured [?], and decode reads ~1.8x the bytes of the 4B (5.67 vs 3.18 GiB [C]). Whether a 4B-to-9B step moves S3/S2 on our corpus is untested [?]. Adopt only if the 4B replay shows capacity, not prompt calibration, is the bottleneck. The unsloth template makes the 9B think-off by default [E, verifier], but still pin it.
- **Rejected:**
  - Qwen3-VL-4B Q4_K_M as the fallback: its stack is measured at 7.02-7.06 GiB with 1.4 GiB spare, but it fails S5 as shipped (8 of 13 truncated at max_tokens 1,024, runaway generation hypothesised), and Qwen3.5-4B has the same size class, 4.5x cheaper KV and newer vendor numbers [E/C]. Kept only as a no-engine-change step-down.
  - Gemma-4-E4B (QAT q4_0 + mmproj or unsloth Q4_K_M): fits (about 6.9-7.6 GiB whole stack [C]), but vendor MMMU-Pro is 52.6 vs 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B (Google's own card vs Qwen's, so only a direction). Cross-vendor Liquid table, RealWorldQA 61.8 vs 76.2 [E]. The weights are 4.6-5.2 GiB because of per-layer embeddings, so it saves no VRAM over the 4B. It needs the same b11090-class pin, with vision fixes landing as late as 2026-09-04 (#28335) and json_schema bugs #23990/#23993 still unresolved [?]. Keep only as a different-lineage control if the Qwen3.5 calibration looks poor.
  - Gemma-4-12B at Q3_K_M or UD-Q3_K_XL: 3-bit is the most quality-damaging trade here, and the SWA KV figure is the softest number in the survey (0.7-1.3 GiB [?]). The gemma4uv path is the least mature in llama.cpp: #24310 CUDA illegal access on the official QAT file on a 16 GB RTX, #29386 silent SIGKILL under image traffic on a 12 GB RTX, and the shipped UBATCH_SIZE=512 hits the non-causal ubatch assert for images over 512 tokens [E]. Q4_K_M is 8.34-9.43 GiB, over budget.
  - Nemotron-Nano-12B-v2-VL (community Q4_K_M + BF16 mmproj): files alone are 8.56 GiB (6.99 + 1.57), over the 8.5 GiB budget by themselves; whole stack about 10.0-10.9 GiB [C]. It is a community GGUF needing b11090, and on llama.cpp it encodes a fixed 256 tokens per image, which under-resolves 1080p stills [E]. Fits 16 GB, not 10. Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni needs 24+ GiB.
  - Cosmos3-Edge (nvidia, 4B): vLLM-only, with no GGUF and no llama.cpp support found [E]. The INT4-AWQ is a TensorRT-Edge-LLM/Jetson artefact that vLLM and transformers refuse, and NF4 is a research artefact. The card recommends --max-model-len 131072 (about 14 GiB of f16 KV for one sequence) and thinking defaults on, and says only BF16 is tested. vLLM f16 KV at 2x16k is about 8.8 GiB plus stack, over 8.5 [C]. Benchmarks are NVIDIA-reported physical-AI numbers, not home stills. A Phase-4 video watch item, not a 10 GB pick.
  - Cosmos-Reason2-2B and 8B: gated:auto, and the 8B loops under our JSON schema (repo-measured). NVIDIA recommends max_tokens 4,096 with <think>, which collides with our 1,024-token budget. GGUFs are single-author community uploads, and the 2B card is unreadable without login [E].
  - Ministral-3-8B (and 3B): the ggml-org GGUF is Q8_0 only, and bartowski's Q4_K_M + f16 mmproj is 8.70 GiB VLM-only, about 9.5 GiB whole stack at 2 slots [C]. Dense KV is 2.26 GiB, with the same cost class as Qwen3-VL-8B. The card advises ~1:1 images (ours are 16:9), the same vendor hedged 11/13 'uncertain' in our bake-off, and its only vision number is MM MTBench [E/R]. No evidence it beats the shipped model.
  - MiniCPM-V-4.5 and InternVL3.5-8B: both are Qwen3-8B-backbone dense models with the same 2.39 GiB KV as the shipped 8B, so no density gain, and MiniCPM-V-4.5 is 9.7 GiB whole stack at 2 slots [C]. The MiniCPM-V-4.5 GGUF carries the separate MiniCPM Model License text (unread [?]). InternVL3.5 is not in llama.cpp's documented support list (InternVL 2.5/3 only), its GGUFs are community-made, and it is about 13 months old [E]. InternVL3.5-4B trails Qwen3.5-4B in the Liquid table (MMStar 65.3 vs 73.3).
  - GLM-4.6V-Flash (10.3B, MIT): runs on the shipped b7972 (PR #18042 predates it) and has cheap KV (0.66 GiB), but at 10 GB only Q3_K_M fits (about 7.8 GiB whole stack [C]); Q4_K_M is about 8.9 GiB. The card admits it 'may overthink or repeat itself', recommends top_k 2 and rep-penalty 1.1 with 16K max tokens, and lists counting and person-identification weaknesses [E]. Keep only as a b7972-compatible alternate if Qwen3.5 fails on S5.
  - LFM2.5-VL-3B: tiny and its arch and projector exist at b7972, but tiling (PR #19454) landed one day after b7972. License is LFM Open License v1.0, commercial use only under USD 10M annual revenue, and MMMU-Pro is 30.5 vs 60.9 for Qwen3.5-4B in the same vendor table, with the card recommending against reasoning-heavy tasks [E].
  - Qwen3.5-2B, Qwen3-VL-2B, MiniCPM-V-4.6 (0.8B LLM): fit trivially (about 3-5 GiB) but are floor models. Qwen3.5-2B non-thinking HallusionBench is 51.3, a bad S2 proxy, and the 2B is loop-prone in thinking mode [E]. MiniCPM-V-4.6 is probably too small for calibrated 0-100 scoring [?]. Sub-10 GB fallbacks only.
  - Mage-VL, OneStreamer-4B, JoyAI-VL-Interaction, Step3-VL-10B (8.38 GiB files alone), granite-vision-4.1-4b (granite4_vision load crash #29780 open), Hawkeye/AnomalyThink fine-tunes: Mage-VL has no mainline llama.cpp or vLLM (both PRs closed unmerged); OneStreamer-4B is Qwen3-VL-4B-shaped and inherits its S5 risk; the rest are off-task or oversized. Hawkeye (2B Qwen3.5 LoRA on ~2.5K synthetic samples, +26.6 pp in-distribution, self-reported) is useful only as evidence the LoRA path is feasible at 2B.
  - No official small (<=9B) Qwen3.6 or Qwen3.8 VL exists on HF, and no security- or surveillance-specific VLM was found for this tier [E: author listings and searches].
- **VRAM math [C]:** Budget = 0.85 x 10 GB = 8.5 GiB for the WHOLE stack (S1: detector, reid and VLM resident, no offload). Method [C]: total = GGUF + mmproj bytes / 2^30 + KV (2 slots x 16,384 = 32,768 cells, q8_0 = 34/32 B per element) + recurrent state + 1.12 GiB buffers + 0.77 GiB detector/reid. The 1.12 + 0.77 = 1.884 GiB constant was back-solved from our own measurement: Qwen3-VL-4B Q4_K_M whole-stack peak 7,192 MiB minus files 2,381.6 + 433 MiB, KV 2,448 MiB and residents 784 MiB = 1,145 MiB. It reproduces the shipped 8B stack at 9.65 GiB vs 9.38-9.60 measured (conservative by 0.05-0.27). It was calibrated at the 1,280-token cap on Qwen3-VL only. PRIMARY Qwen3.5-4B Q8_0: weights 4.175 + mmproj F16 0.626 = 4.801 GiB. KV: 8 full-attention layers x 4 KV heads x 256 hd x 2 (K,V) x 2 B = 32,768 B/token f16 x 32,768 cells = 1.000 GiB f16 x 0.53125 = 0.531 GiB. Recurrent state: 24 DeltaNet layers at ~49 MiB/slot x 2 = 0.098 GiB (assumes f32 state [?]). Total = 4.801 + 0.531 + 0.098 + 1.884 = 7.31 GiB, spare 1.19. The verifiers' lower basis (0.8 GiB buffers instead of 1.12) gives 6.23 GiB VLM-only, or 7.00 with the 0.77 residents. The scout's 7.31 is therefore the conservative end of 7.0-7.3. Stress case: if flash-attn must be off with f16 KV (the #25717 workaround) add 0.47 GiB, and a 248k-vocab logits buffer plausibly adds ~0.3, giving about 8.1 GiB, still under 8.5. Q6_K = 6.42 GiB; Q4_K_M = 5.69 GiB. Raising the image cap by +5,120 tokens adds only ~85 MiB of KV on this arch, but the vision-encoder scratch for a higher cap is unmeasured [?]. Each extra 16k slot costs ~0.32 GiB. AMBITIOUS Qwen3.5-9B IQ4_XS: 4.814 + 0.855 = 5.669 files + 0.629 KV/state + 1.884 = 8.18 GiB (margin 0.32; 7.87 on the lower basis). Q4_K_M: 6.145 files + 0.629 + 1.884 = 8.66 (2 slots, misses) or 8.35 (1 slot). FALLBACK Qwen3-VL-8B Q4_K_M + mmproj Q8_0: files 4.682 + 0.701 = 5.383 GiB; 1-slot q8_0 KV 1,224 MiB = 1.195 GiB; formula 8.46 GiB; measurement-subtracted 9,606 - 1,224 = 8,382 MiB = 8.19 GiB; margin 0.04-0.31. Weights use file bytes, which overstate llama.cpp GPU residency by a few percent (token embeddings stay on CPU) [E]. All figures are [C]; none was measured on a 10 GB card, and the only measured card is the 24 GB A5500, which shares sm_86 with the RTX 3080.
- **Measure first on our corpus:** Nothing below is an accuracy claim; only fit and support are established. Replay each check on the existing synthbench harness (tierb-v0, 450 FLUX.2 stills, declared truth, ideal-detector rows; add a models.py row for the candidate) with the same prompt, max_tokens 1,024 and 4 x 1,280-token images. Run the shipped Qwen3-VL-8B on the SAME new build as the control: S3 36.1-36.5%, S2 6.7-9.1%, S5 1/450, S4 p95 16.2 s. (1) Fit gate. Build b11090-class with CUDA arch 86 and load the primary. Record llama.cpp's 'projected' line and the nvidia-smi whole-stack peak at 250 ms with both slots busy, uncapped and capped. Pass if <= 8.5 GiB (a peak measured on the A5500 transfers to a 3080 apart from driver reserve). A real 3080 run is better. (2) Enforcement probe. Run S-2 json_schema ENFORCED with images, thinking explicitly OFF via enable_thinking=false or --reasoning off, plus the tool probe, and re-check that the probe budget (1,024 + 64) does not truncate it. (3) S5: refusals and truncations at 1,024 tokens on the 13-item gen-2 set and on tierb-v0 (the Qwen3-VL-4B sibling failed 8/13; target ~0). (4) S2 and S3 with confidence intervals and per-scenario slices. S3 means recall at or above the declared band (>= 90% bar); S2 means benign false alarms (<= 5%), including the hard negatives (hooded_jogger, power_tools_at_night, flashlight_neighbor) and the S3 weak scenarios (child_alone_at_pool, package_theft, knife_visible, fire_or_smoke). Record the fraction of incident scores below band (shipped: 62.7%, 54 pts). Add a prompt/threshold control, because model family and prompt calibration are confounded. (5) S4 p95 <= 30 s on the same card with 4-image prefill, plus prompt-cache behaviour with the hybrid recurrent state at 2 slots (latency cost, not VRAM). (6) Vision correctness: compare FA-on vs FA-off image embeddings or answers on sm_86 (#29629), and run a 2-slot soak with mixed text and image turns (#27931, #28166 M-RoPE warnings). (7) Only if S1 margin shrinks, A/B Q8_0 vs Q6_K vs Q4_K_M; and run the 9B IQ4_XS only if the 4B replay suggests capacity, not calibration, is the limit.
- **Open questions:**
  - [?] No candidate except Qwen3-VL has any tierb-v0 measurement; every Qwen3.5 benchmark is vendor-run on public VQA, not security stills or calibrated risk scoring. S2/S3 for the primary are completely unknown.
  - [?] The real overhead constant for qwen35 on our build: the calibrated 1.12 GiB buffer was measured at the 1,280-token cap on Qwen3-VL only, while verifiers used 0.8 GiB. Qwen3.5 has a 248k vocab (larger logits buffer), an F16 mmproj and an unmeasured vision scratch for any cap above 1,280. Whole-stack Q8_0 could land anywhere in about 7.0-8.1 GiB until measured.
  - [?] The recurrent-state size and slot behaviour of the hybrid Qwen3.5 under 2 parallel slots with llama-server (f32 state assumed, prompt-cache reuse degrades per #21831/#26994). Verify the 'projected' and nvidia-smi lines.
  - [?] Whether llama.cpp #20345 (grammar not enforced with thinking on) is truly fixed on the chosen build, and whether Qwen3.5-4B stays under 1,024 tokens (S5) with thinking off. The unsloth GGUF template is off by default [E], but another GGUF repo might not be.
  - [?] The first llama.cpp build with qwen35 support lies between b8000 and b8200 and was not bisected. b11090 predates the Qwen3.5 LoRA-to-GGUF conversion fix (#28324, 2026-09-30) and the Muse/other fixes. Choose the pin deliberately; the latest tag seen is b11376 (2026-10-03). Moving the pin means re-qualifying the Qwen3-VL-8B control and the S-2 probe, and rebuilding for sm_86.
  - [?] #29629 (CUDA FA gives wrong mmproj embeddings, grows with image size, reported on sm_86 and on Qwen3.5-4B, open, fix branch unproven) may also be depressing the shipped Qwen3-VL-8B S3. It is cheap to rule out before attributing the 36% to the model.
  - [?] No Q8_0 mmproj exists for Qwen3.5; self-quantizing it (+0.2 GiB saved) is unverified, and the effect of mmproj F16 vs Q8_0 on S3 is unmeasured.
  - [?] The measured margin of the fallback (0.04-0.31 GiB) has never been observed on a 10 GB card, and the 1-slot setting serializes concurrent cameras. Desktop/driver reserve on a real RTX 3080 may eat the margin.
  - [?] Whether quantization level (Q8_0 vs Q4/IQ4_XS) matters for calibration-sensitive scoring more than for extraction accuracy is unmeasured; the evidence suggests the S3 gap is prompt/calibration, so a calibration-targeted LoRA on the 4B (trained on a bigger card, merged, then quantized) is the plausible lever. No published security benchmark matches our still-image verdict-plus-risk task, so tierb-v0 stays the primary benchmark.
  - [?] Licence/legal items not read: Qwen3.5 is apache-2.0 (confirmed by tag), but Gemma, MiniCPM and Cosmos terms listed above were not read in full.

### 12 GB card (budget 10.2 GiB)

- **Primary:** Qwen/Qwen3.5-9B - GGUF Q4_K_M (unsloth/Qwen3.5-9B-GGUF, 5,680,522,464 B) + mmproj-F16 (918,166,080 B; no Q8_0 mmproj exists) [E] - **8.67 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp newer than the shipped b7972 (qwen35 arch is absent at b7972, present at b11090; minimum build lies somewhere between b8000 and b8200, not bisected) [E]; llama-server with thinking off, FA on, q8_0 KV, PARALLEL 2 x 16k
  - Why: Recommendation on fit, runtime support and vendor priors only. Nothing here has been measured on tierb-v0 [?]. Apache-2.0, ungated, pipeline image-text-to-text [E].

(1) Fit. Only 8 of 32 layers hold a KV cache; the other 24 are Gated-DeltaNet. A 16k slot costs about 0.31 GiB, against 1.195 GiB for the shipped Qwen3-VL-8B [C]. Whole stack is about 8.3-8.7 GiB (see vramMath). The shipped 8B measured 9.38-9.83 GiB [E: repo, A5500]. Headroom to the 10.2 GiB budget is 1.5-1.9 GiB, versus 0.4-0.8 GiB today. Spare room buys about 4 more slots, or Q5_K_M (+0.84 GiB, whole stack about 9.2-9.5). Q6_K leaves essentially nothing and Q8_0 does not fit [C].

(2) Vendor priors (self-reported, not security, likely thinking mode). Qwen3.5-9B vs the Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B column: RealWorldQA 80.3 vs 77.4, HallusionBench 69.3 vs 66.0, CountBench 97.2 vs 90.0, MMStar 79.7 vs 75.5 [E]. The table has no Qwen3-VL-8B column, so 'better than our shipped 8B' is inference [?].

(3) Same Qwen family and the same patch16/merge2 vision geometry (32 px per token). Not identical: Qwen3.5 has no deepstack indexes and ships a larger F16 mmproj, so the vision graph differs and the 1280-token cap must be re-verified [E/?]. The LoRA path stays in one ecosystem. The Qwen3.5 LoRA-to-GGUF conversion fix (#28324) merged 2026-09-30, after b11090, so plan to merge the adapter and then quantize [E].

This is NOT a claim that S3 or S2 improves. Our flagship reached only S3 58.9%, and 62.7% of Qwen incident scores fall below the declared band by about 54 points [E: repo]. That is a calibration/prompt problem a model swap may not fix. More VRAM at this tier buys quant, slots and image-token cap, and no evidence says it buys accuracy. The only same-family ladder read was Qwen3.5 4B to 9B, worth about +0.8 to +6 vendor points [E].

Depends on a runtime we do not ship (second llama.cpp pin). Extra work: (a) the repo already builds ai-vlm:sm103-b11090 for GB300, so rebuild for the target sm_86 (3060), sm_89 (4070) or sm_120 (5070); (b) set thinking off server-side, because the shipped client and Dockerfile send nothing; the unsloth GGUF's embedded template reportedly defaults to off while the model card says on [E], so verify it; (c) re-run the json_schema enforcement probe, since grammar was not enforced with thinking on (#20345) [E]; (d) add a models.py row; (e) bump the VLM_REQUIRED_BUILD pin. Not vLLM: Qwen3.5-9B AWQ is 9.07-12.38 GB and NVFP4 is 9.36 GB, before KV and engine overhead, and FP4 tensor cores exist only on sm_120 [E].

- **Fallback:** Qwen/Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct-GGUF - Q4_K_M (5,027,784,800 B) + mmproj Q8_0 (752,289,728 B) = 5.383 GiB [E], exactly as shipped - **9.49 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp b7972 as shipped (no engine, client or prompt change)
  - Why: Closest to what is proven on our stack. Measured whole stack on the 24 GB RTX A5500 at 2 slots x 16k q8_0: 9.38 GiB corpus peak, 9.49 GiB capped full-slot (9,722 MiB, the figure in totalGiB) and 9.83 GiB uncapped [E: repo]. Against 10.2 GiB that leaves only 0.4-0.8 GiB. No measurement exists on any 12 GB card, and the desktop/display may hold VRAM there [?]. At PARALLEL 1, q8_0 KV drops by 1.195 GiB, to about 8.2-8.6 GiB [C].

Proven on this stack [E: repo]: S4 p95 16.2 s against the 30 s bar; S5 zero unparseable and zero refusals on 13 gen-2 items and 1 truncation of 450 on tierb-v0; json_schema ENFORCED; 37/37 layers on GPU, no offload. Known failures: S3 36.1-36.5% (FAIL) and S2 6.7-9.1% (marginal/fail).

It is the control arm for any A/B, not a fix. Qwen3-VL-4B Q8_0 was considered for the 12 GB card and not chosen: the 4B truncated 8/13 gen-2 items at max_tokens 1024 at Q4_K_M, and whether Q8_0 cures that is untested [?].

- **Ambitious:** google/gemma-4-12B-it, official Google QAT GGUF google/gemma-4-12B-it-qat-q4_0-gguf: gemma-4-12b-it-qat-q4_0.gguf 6,975,879,296 B + mmproj 175,115,616 B = 6.66 GiB [E]; the encoder-free 12B has a tiny mmproj. Apache-2.0, ungated. Pipeline tag is any-to-any, so an image-text-to-text search will miss it [E].\n\nFit [C]. With sliding-window KV and without --swa-full, KV is about 0.68-0.76 GiB at q8_0 (assumed, not read from llama.cpp source). VLM only is about 8.1-8.4 GiB; adding the 0.77 GiB stack gives about 8.9-9.2 GiB. The conservative scout calibration gives 9.5-10.26 GiB, borderline against 10.2. Never set --swa-full: it adds about 4.6-4.9 GiB of KV and breaks the fit.\n\nCapability prior (vendor, self-reported): MMMU-Pro 69.1 versus 52.6 for E4B; it is the largest dense model that sits on the 12 GB line [E].\n\nWhy ambitious, not primary: it is the least mature llama.cpp vision path.\n- #24310: CUDA illegal memory access on the QAT Q4_0 file (RTX 4080 SUPER), closed stale and unfixed.\n- #29386: silent SIGKILL under sustained image traffic on an RTX 3060 12 GB, with a different quant and an MTP draft. It was closed by PR #29773 on 2026-10-01, which caps image tokens to n_ubatch. That fix is after b11090.\n- Gemma 4 vision needs n_ubatch >= image tokens, but the repo ships UBATCH_SIZE=512. Raising -ub to 1120-2048 grows the compute buffer beyond the 0.8 GiB assumed.\n- #29735: roughly 4 s reallocation on the first large image.\n- json_schema is unverified; the prefill fix #23993 is still open, and an empty thought block is emitted even with thinking off.\n\nNeeds a build after 2026-10-01 and a soak test before any scoring. Note that Qwen3.5-9B Q5_K_M with a higher image-token cap is a lower-risk way to spend the same spare VRAM.
- **Rejected:**
  - nvidia Nemotron-Nano-12B-v2-VL Q4_K_M + BF16 mmproj (community GGUF): VLM only 10.04 GiB [C] (7.50 GB weights + 1.69 GB mmproj + KV + Mamba state), about 10.8 GiB with the stack. That is over 10.2 [C]. On llama.cpp the projector is a fixed 256 tokens per image, tiling PR #23638 is unmerged and #25317 closed stale, so 1080p stills are under-resolved [E]. Needs b11090; GGUF is community-made; nvidia-open-model-license. Fits 16 GB only.
  - Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni-30B-A3B: 22-24.5 GiB at Q4 [C]. Nowhere near 12 GB.
  - Ministral-3-8B-Instruct-2512 Q4_K_M: about 8.7 GiB VLM only, 9.5-9.9 GiB with stack [C]. KV is dense GQA (2.26 GiB q8_0), so it gains nothing over the shipped 8B. Same Mistral lineage as the Small-3.2 that hedged 11/13 'uncertain' [E: repo]. Vendor evidence is MM MTBench only. Card advises ~1:1 aspect, and our stills are 16:9. json_schema on llama.cpp is untested, and PR #21537 on dropped response_format is still open.
  - MiniCPM-V-4.5 (Qwen3-8B backbone): 9.66-9.86 GiB whole stack [C]. Same KV as the shipped 8B (2.39 GiB), mmproj F16 only, and the GGUF repo carries no license tag and defers to a separate MiniCPM Model License that I did not read [E/?]. It tests the vision-tower axis, not the density axis.
  - GLM-4.6V-Flash Q4_K_M + Q8_0 mmproj (MIT): VLM only 8.12 GiB, about 8.9-9.1 GiB with stack [C]. KV is only 0.66 GiB. It runs on the SHIPPED b7972 (PR #18042 merged 2025-12-16, before b7972) [E]. This is the only engine-unchanged non-Qwen3-VL option, but it is deprioritised, not fit-rejected. The card admits it 'may overthink or repeat itself' and recommends top_k 2, rep-penalty 1.1 and 16K tokens, which is the Cosmos/4B S5 truncation pattern. Counting and person identification are listed limitations, and thinking cannot be confirmed switched off [E/?]. Cheap smoke test only if the second llama.cpp pin is unacceptable.
  - Qwen3.5-4B (Q8_0 about 7.2 GiB whole stack, 6.2 VLM only) [C]: a sound 10 GB-tier pick, but at 12 GB it gives up capability for spare room we cannot yet use. Vendor gap to 9B is small (MMMU-Pro 66.3 vs 70.1, HallusionBench 65.0 vs 69.3) [E]. Keep it as the fast alternate if the 9B misses S4.
  - Step3-VL-10B: Q4_K_M + F16 mmproj is 8.38 GiB of files alone (the 3.97 GB vision encoder is 44% of weights), about 11.6-12.5 GiB total [C]. Over 10.2. Reasoning-first model, community GGUF, and the vendor admitted benchmark errors.
  - Ministral-3-14B (11.95-13.1 GiB), Gemma-4-12B at Q5_K_M and above (more than 11 GiB [C]), Qwen3.5-9B at Q6_K/Q8_0 (about 10.0 and 12.2 GiB whole stack): no margin or over budget [C].
  - Gemma-4-E4B QAT (6.8-8.2 GiB) and MiniCPM-V-4.6 (0.8B LLM, about 2.8 GiB): fit easily but too weak for 0-100 risk calibration. E4B is MMMU-Pro 52.6 vs Qwen3.5-4B 66.3 in separate vendor tables [E]. LFM2.5-VL-3B adds a USD 10M revenue license threshold and weak reasoning (MMMU-Pro 30.5).
  - vLLM and quant-format paths (Qwen3.5-9B AWQ/NVFP4/FP8, Gemma-4-E4B w4a16 11.5 GB, Cosmos3-Edge, Cosmos3-Nano reasoner): they do not fit 12 GB once KV and engine overhead are added, or they have no llama.cpp path [C]. Cosmos3-Edge is BF16-only per its card; its INT4-AWQ targets TensorRT-Edge-LLM on Jetson and refuses vLLM; its default 131072 context needs 14 GiB of f16 KV [E].
  - Cosmos-Reason2-2B/8B and Cosmos3-Nano GGUF: the repo already measured Cosmos-Reason2-8B looping under the JSON schema [E: repo]. Cosmos-Reason2 repos are gated; the Cosmos3-Nano GGUF is a one-author tensor re-key.
  - InternVL3.5-8B: llama.cpp documents support only through InternVL3, and the 3.5 GGUFs are community-made. Older and superseded [E].
  - No Qwen3.6/3.8 VLM at 9B or smaller exists from Qwen (only 27B and 35B-A3B and up); the small 'Qwen3.8' hits are third-party distills or text-only [E]. No security- or surveillance-specific VLM is usable: CamVLM-8B is gated and unreadable (BF16 only, about 19.5 GiB), AnomalyThink is industrial, and HolmesVAU/Vad-R1 are video-only [E].
- **VRAM math [C]:** Budget: 0.85 x 12 = 10.2 GiB for the whole stack, no VLM CPU offload, 2 slots x 16,384 = 32,768 KV cells, q8_0 = 34/32 B per element [C].

Primary: Qwen3.5-9B Q4_K_M + F16 mmproj [E sizes, C sums]

- Weights: 5,680,522,464 B = 5.290 GiB.
- mmproj-F16: 918,166,080 B = 0.855 GiB. The unsloth repo has no Q8_0 mmproj, so a locally quantized one could save about 0.4 GiB [?].
- KV: 8 full-attention layers x 4 KV heads x 256 head_dim x 2 (K,V) = 16,384 elements per token. Times 34/32 B is 17,408 B per token. Times 32,768 cells is 0.531 GiB.
- Gated-DeltaNet recurrent state: 24 layers x about 2.2 MB x 2 slots = 0.098 GiB (assumes an f32 state layout, not read from llama.cpp [?]).
- Buffers: 0.8 GiB. Calibration: the same formula gives 8.575 GiB for the shipped 8B pair against a measured llama-server peak of 8,822 MiB = 8.615 GiB [E: repo].
- VLM only: 5.290 + 0.855 + 0.629 + 0.8 = 7.57 GiB.
- Add the detector/reid/compositor stack: 0.77 GiB (9,606 - 8,822 MiB measured) = 8.34 GiB whole stack.
- Scout's more conservative overhead term (1.9 GiB, calibrated on the 4B) gives 8.67 GiB. totalGiB uses 8.67.
- Headroom against 10.2 is 1.5-1.9 GiB. At PARALLEL 1 the total falls by about 0.31 GiB.
- Variants (whole stack, 8.34 / 8.67 conventions): Q5_K_M about 9.2 / 9.5; Q6_K about 10.0 / 10.3 (no margin); Q8_0 about 11.2 / 12.2 (over).
- Not included and unmeasured [?]: the mmproj compute buffer at 4 x 1280-token images; whether the 0.8 GiB buffer holds at a higher image-token cap.

Fallback: Qwen3-VL-8B Q4_K_M + Q8_0 mmproj

- 5.383 GiB files + KV 2,448 MiB = 2.391 GiB (36 layers x 8 KV heads x 128 head_dim; matches the repo's measured KV line exactly) + 0.8 buffers = 8.57 GiB VLM only, +0.77 stack = 9.34 GiB.
- Measured on the A5500: 9.38 GiB corpus peak, 9.49 GiB capped full slot, 9.83 GiB uncapped [E: repo]. Headroom is 0.4-0.8 GiB.
- No 12 GB-card measurement exists.

Ambitious: Gemma-4-12B QAT q4_0 + mmproj

- 6.497 + 0.163 = 6.66 GiB files.
- KV 0.68-0.76 GiB at q8_0: 8 global layers x 1 KV head x 512 head_dim over 32,768 cells plus 40 sliding layers x 8 heads x 256 head_dim over 2,560-3,072 cells. This assumes llama.cpp's default SWA cache and that K and V are stored separately [?].
- Plus 0.8 buffers = 8.14-8.4 GiB VLM only. Plus 0.77 stack = 8.9-9.2 GiB.
- Scout's conservative overhead gives 9.5-10.26 GiB. A non-unified 6,144-cell SWA cache gives KV of 1.26 GiB and a total of 8.72 GiB.
- Does not include the larger compute buffer when -ub is raised to match the image tokens.

Rejected on this math: Nemotron-12B VLM only is 10.04 GiB (6.987 + 1.573 + 0.398 + 0.281 Mamba state + 0.8), +0.77 = 10.8 GiB, over 10.2.

All estimates are fit-only [C]. None has been run on a 12 GB card.

- **Measure first on our corpus:** Gate 0, before any scoring. Run the candidate on a real 12 GB card, RTX 3060 (sm_86), 4070 (sm_89) or 5070 (sm_120). Only the 24 GB A5500 has ever been measured. Use a fresh llama.cpp build with the correct CUDA arch (the repo ships arch 89 in .env.example). Keep the shipped shape: CTX 32768, PARALLEL 2, q8_0 KV, flash-attn on, LLAMA_ARG_IMAGE_MAX_TOKENS 1280, UBATCH 512. Record these:\n(a) llama-server 'projected' and 'llama_kv_cache: size =' lines, and the nvidia-smi 250 ms whole-stack peak with detector, reid and the VLM resident. S1 passes if the peak is <= 10.2 GiB with all layers on GPU and no CPU offload.\n(b) Thinking off, set server-side (--chat-template-kwargs enable_thinking=false or --reasoning off / --reasoning-budget 0). Confirm no '<think>' text in replies, and no VlmTruncatedError at max_tokens 1024 over 4-image calls. The 4B Qwen3-VL failed this at 8/13 [E: repo].\n(c) json_schema response_format ENFORCED with images and thinking off, plus the tool probe. Check the 400/464-token probe-budget cliff. Also confirm the 1280-token image cap applies to this mmproj, which has no deepstack layers.\n(d) Soak: 2 slots, alternating text and image turns, FA on plus q8_0 KV. Compare vision embeddings with FA on and off (open issues #29629, #28166, #29830, #25717; the last is especially relevant on sm_120).\n\nThen replay tierb-v0 through the existing synthbench harness. Add models.py rows for the new GGUF on the new image, with the same prompt and the same ideal-detector rows, and re-run the shipped Qwen3-VL-8B on the same new build as a regression control. Score:\n- S3 incident recall >= 90%. Shipped is 36.1-36.5%. Also record per-scenario hits (package_theft and child_alone_at_pool were 0%, fire/knife 52.6%) and the share of incident scores below the declared band (62.7%, mean 54 points under).\n- S2 false alarms <= 5%. Shipped is 6.7-9.1%. Watch the hard negatives: hooded_jogger and power_tools_at_night.\n- S5 refusals about zero, split into refusals vs length truncations. Shipped had 1 truncation in 450.\n- S4 p95 <= 30 s. Shipped is 16.2 s on an A5500 (768 GB/s class). 3060/4070 memory bandwidth is much lower [A, not fetched], so S4 may degrade; the dense 9B reads 5.68 GB per token against 5.03 GB.\n\nTo isolate a family effect from the prompt, add a prompt/threshold control arm on the shipped 8B. The S3 misses are conservative scoring, so any gain is a 'model effect' only if it survives that control. Nothing in this recommendation is a claim of task accuracy, only of fit and support.
- **Open questions:**
  - Minimum llama.cpp build for qwen35 plus the qwen3vl_merger mmproj: b7972 lacks it, b8200 and later have it, and the first-supporting tag between b8000 and b8200 was not bisected. Pin a current build (latest seen b11376, 2026-10-03), not b11090, so the LoRA conversion fix (#28324, 2026-09-30) is included [E/?].
  - Real VRAM peak on a 12 GB card with a display attached. The fit math is calibrated only on the A5500, and the 0.8 GiB buffer term has not been measured for hybrid models or the F16 mmproj [?].
  - Whether the unsloth GGUF's embedded chat template really defaults to thinking off, and how presence_penalty 1.5 (the card's instruct-mode setting) interacts with schema-constrained JSON [?].
  - Whether the FA-on + q8_0 KV + M-RoPE vision crash seen on a Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on sm_120 (#25717, closed stale, unfixed) reproduces on 9B dense on a 5070-class 12 GB card [?]. Also whether CUDA FA gives wrong vision embeddings (#29629), which could affect the shipped Qwen3-VL-8B as well.
  - S4 on 12 GB-class memory bandwidth. No GPU spec was fetched, so any S4 estimate is [?].
  - Gemma-4-12B only: which build first includes #29773 (image cap to ubatch); how far -ub must rise above the shipped 512; whether llama.cpp sizes the SWA cache as assumed (0.68 vs about 5.6 GiB with --swa-full); and json_schema behaviour with the empty thought block [?].
  - Whether a locally quantized Q8_0 mmproj for Qwen3.5-9B loads and keeps vision quality. It would save about 0.4 GiB but is unverified [?].
  - Whether the Qwen3.5 LoRA-to-GGUF path works for the linear_attention projections the adapters target. Train with HF/PEFT on a bigger card and merge before quantizing [?]. A published UCF-Crime LoRA on Qwen3.8-27B showed dAUROC about +0.02-0.03 with all three CIs including 0 and false positives per hour rising from 29 to 92, so a weak-label LoRA could worsen S2 [E].
  - No security-scene benchmark exists for any candidate here; every vendor number is general VQA and self-reported. tierb-v0 stays the primary benchmark, and 'Harbor' is not a VLM benchmark in anything I read [E/?].

### 16 GB card (budget 13.6 GiB)

- **Primary:** unsloth/Qwen3.5-9B-GGUF (base: Qwen/Qwen3.5-9B) - Q6_K + mmproj-F16 (ladder: Q4_K_M 8.7 / Q6_K 10.3 / Q8_0 12.2 GiB; test Q8_0 as the one-variable step-up) - **10.3 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp newer than shipped b7972: qwen35 arch is absent at b7972 and b8000 and present from b8200 through b11090 [E]. The first supporting build was not bisected [?]. The repo already builds b11090 (ai-vlm:sm103-b11090), but only for sm_103; a 16 GB card needs a rebuild for sm_89 (4060 Ti/4080) or sm_120 (5070 Ti/5080). Latest release seen is b11376. Run with FA on, q8_0 KV, 2 slots x 16k, --image-max-tokens 1280, and thinking explicitly off (--reasoning off or chat_template_kwargs enable_thinking=false).
  - Why: Fit and support only. Nothing here is a claim of accuracy on our task. (1) Closest lineage to the shipped model: same vendor, apache-2.0, ungated. Vision uses the qwen3vl_merger projector, patch16/merge2 = 32 px per token, so the 1280-token cap should map [E source-level, not run]. The mmproj graph is not identical: deepstack_visual_indexes is [] vs [8,16,24] on Qwen3-VL-8B [E]. (2) Cheapest cache of any candidate that fits: only 8 of 32 layers hold KV (4 KV heads x 256), so KV is 0.53 GiB at q8_0 for 2x16k vs 2.39 GiB for the shipped 8B, plus ~0.1 GiB DeltaNet state [C]. Each extra 16k slot costs ~0.31 GiB, and raising the image cap costs only ~85 MiB of KV per +5,120 tokens [C]. (3) Vendor card (self-reported, general VQA, not security stills): MMMU-Pro 70.1, RealWorldQA 80.3, HallusionBench 69.3, CountBench 97.2, VlmsAreBlind 93.7, versus Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B at 63.0/77.4/66.0/90.0/72.5 [E]. There is no Qwen3-VL-8B column, so 9B vs the shipped 8B is an inference [C]. (4) Why Q6_K rather than Q8_0: decode is memory-bound. Q8_0 reads ~9.5 GB per token (1.9x the shipped 5.03 GB) and Q6_K reads ~7.5 GB (1.5x). The shipped 8B measured S4 p95 16.2 s on the 768 GB/s A5500, and 30 s is the bar. Pessimistic bytes scaling [C] gives ~31 s for Q8_0 and ~24 s for Q6_K on A5500-class bandwidth. A 4060 Ti 16 GB at 288 GB/s [A] is ~2.7x slower again. Vendor-reported quant loss is small (NVIDIA and Meta report <=1 point), so extra bits are the least valuable use of spare VRAM. Q6_K also keeps ~3.3 GiB of headroom against the unmeasured buffer terms. (5) Known landmines for this family: thinking is on by default per the card, and llama.cpp #20345 reports grammar NOT enforced with thinking on (tested on 35B-A3B). The unsloth GGUF embeds an inverted template that is thinking OFF unless enable_thinking=true, but other GGUFs may differ; pin the repo and send enable_thinking=false explicitly. mmproj ships only as F16/BF16/F32 (no Q8_0), costing +0.15 GiB vs the shipped Q8_0 mmproj; self-quantizing it is possible but unverified [?]. Open llama.cpp issues on this family: #28166 (mrope warnings per image; noise vs corruption unestablished), #29629 (CUDA FA gives wrong mmproj embeddings, also affects the shipped Qwen3-VL path), #29830 (NaN recurrent state sticks across requests), #25717/#27931 (hybrid + mmproj crashes, reported on the 35B-A3B MoE, FA + q8_0 KV on sm_120). The 4B/9B dense models have no crash report found, but our config sits in that crash matrix [?]. Extra work: move the VLM image pin, rebuild for the target sm, re-run the S-2 json_schema enforcement probe with images, and add synthbench models.py rows. models.yml has no VLM row today.
- **Fallback:** Qwen/Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct-GGUF (shipped model) - Q4_K_M + mmproj Q8_0, exactly as shipped (5,027,784,800 + 752,289,728 B). Optional single-variable step-ups: Q6_K (unsloth, pair with the official Q8_0 mmproj) 11.3 GiB, or Q8_0 + mmproj Q8_0 13.1 GiB (only 0.5 GiB under budget, thin). - **9.6 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp b7972 (shipped pin, no engine change, no new probe). Rebuild for the 16 GB card's sm only (.env.example ships 89; sm_120 5070 Ti/5080 need a rebuild).
  - Why: This is the only model with real S1-S5 numbers on tierb-v0 and the stack. [R/E] Whole-stack peak is measured at 9.38-9.83 GiB on the 24 GB A5500, with 37/37 layers on GPU and no offload. It fits the 13.6 GiB budget with ~3.8 GiB headroom. [E] The official GGUF byte counts match the repo's recorded files exactly. Measured on tierb-v0: S3 36.1-36.5% (FAIL vs 90%), S2 6.7-9.1% (marginal/fail), S4 p95 16.2 s (pass), S5 0 refusals on gen-2 and 1 length truncation in 450 (pass). No 16 GB card has been measured [?]. Use it as the regression control on the SAME build as the primary, to separate engine/flag effects from model effects. It is NOT a fix for S3: ~63% of its incident scores sit below the declared band by ~54 points (conservative scoring, not missed objects), and the larger flagship reached only 58.9%. The Q8_0 step-up is a free file swap, but quant is unlikely to move calibration [?]. It is also the model with the most LoRA tooling, since the same Qwen LoRA stack applies.
- **Ambitious:** Qwen3.8-27B in the ISTA-DASLab GSQ-RCO 2-bit GGUF (ISTA-DASLab/Qwen3.8-27B-GSQ-RCO-GGUF, IQ2_S 9.26 GB + BF16 mmproj 0.93 GB, apache-2.0, community quantizer). Fit [C]: weights 8.62 GiB + mmproj 0.867 + q8_0 KV 1.06 (16 full-attn layers) + DeltaNet state 0.29 = 10.84, plus 0.8 buffers = 11.65 GiB, or ~12.8-13.2 with the calibrated 1.9 GiB overhead. IQ2_XS is ~1 GiB smaller. IQ3_XXS (12.4-14.0) is over budget at 2 slots, ~13.3 at 1 slot. Why it is ambitious: it is the largest, newest dense Qwen VLM that can sit on 16 GB. Vendor card (self-reported) RealWorldQA 85.9, OmniDocBench 91.1, CharXiv-RQ 83.7, versus 80.3 for Qwen3.5-9B [E]. Why it is risky: (a) the quant card's evals are text-only (GPQA-D 84.85 at IQ2_XS vs 89.90 BF16), and vision-path degradation at 2-bit is unmeasured [?]; (b) dense 27B decode and a 4-image ~5k-token prefill make S4 the likeliest break on a 16 GB card [C]; (c) thinking is on by default with reasoning_effort defaulting to xhigh, which blows the 1,024-token budget unless disabled; (d) same qwen35 runtime dependencies and open hybrid-vision issues as the primary; (e) the repo evidence (8B 36.5% vs flagship 58.9% S3) says size alone will not reach 90%. Second ambitious option with a different lineage: google/gemma-4-12B-it-qat-q4_0-gguf (official Google QAT, apache-2.0). Fit [C]: weights 6.50 + mmproj 0.16 + KV 0.7-1.3 + buffers 0.8-1.4 + 0.77 stack = 8.7-9.5 GiB; Q6_K (unsloth) 10.8-12.1 GiB. Vendor MMMU-Pro 69.1, MATH-Vision 79.7 [E]. It has the least mature llama.cpp vision path in this survey. #24310: CUDA illegal memory access on the official QAT file on an RTX 4080 SUPER 16 GB, stale-closed and unfixed. #29386: silent SIGKILL under sustained image traffic (a fix PR, #29773, caps image tokens to ubatch and merged after b11090). The shipped UBATCH_SIZE=512 would cap or assert on any image over 512 tokens unless -ub is raised. The F32-accumulate half of #24146 appears unfixed on master. It needs a soak test before it is a serious candidate.
- **Rejected:**
  - google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it (QAT Q4_0): weights + mmproj alone are 14.56 GiB, over the 13.6 GiB budget, so the total is ~16.0-16.3 GiB [C]. A 16 GB card would need CPU expert offload, which violates S1 (no CPU offload of the VLM). Fits 24 GB and up only.
  - Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (UD-IQ2_XXS 13.07 GiB, 0.5 GiB headroom): 2-bit MoE with unmeasured quality, thinking on by default, and open CUDA hybrid + mmproj crash reports (#25717 stale-closed unfixed, #27931 open). Not recommended. Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B (Q3_K_M 17.7 GiB) and Qwen3-VL-32B (Q4_K_M ~24 GiB) also do not fit.
  - NVIDIA Nemotron-Nano-12B-v2-VL (community Q4_K_M 7.50 GB + BF16 mmproj 1.69 GB, ~10.0-10.9 GiB, repo-measured 10,118 MiB at f16 KV): it fits 16 GB and was measured on our harness at n=13 only (S3 7/8, S2 0/5). Rejected as primary because llama.cpp encodes every image at a fixed 256 tokens (nemotron_v2_vl routes to the fixed-size preprocessor; #25317 stale-closed, tiling PR #23638 closed unmerged). A 1080p still is squeezed to one 512 px tile, so tierb-v0 results under-represent the model and small subjects are lost. Also community-only GGUF, needs b11090, nvidia-open-model-license. Keep as the named reopen if KV density binds (its KV is 408 MiB at q8_0). Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni (22.4 GB NVFP4 / 24.2 GB GGUF) does not fit 16 GB at any quant.
  - Ministral-3-14B / 8B: 14B Q4_K_M is 11.95-13.1 GiB (GQA KV 2.66 GiB, thin). The same Mistral lineage as Mistral-Small-3.2 hedged 11/13 'uncertain' on our harness, and mistral3 json_schema returned 500s on b7972. The card advises 1:1 images (ours are 16:9). The only vision evidence is MM MTBench, a chat benchmark. The HF repos carry no pipeline_tag.
  - InternVL3.5-14B (12.5-13.5 GiB, thin) and -8B: InternVL3.5 is not in llama.cpp's documented support list (only 2.5 and 3). The GGUFs are community quants (bartowski, b6258) and untested on our builds. The models are ~13 months old. Dynamic tiling at 256 tokens per tile can blow the vision-token budget. InternVL3.5-30B-A3B (19.3 GB) and -38B (31 GB, 11 GB ViT mmproj) do not fit.
  - Step3-VL-10B (11.6-12.5 GiB): reasoning-first model whose GGUF template forces <think>, with no documented no-think switch. Community-only GGUF with a 4 GB F16 mmproj. Needs a post-b7972 build (PRs #21287/#21646). The vendor disclosed benchmark errors and has not replicated the re-runs. High S4/S5 risk, the pattern that sank Cosmos and Qwen3-VL-4B here.
  - GLM-4.6V-Flash (ggml-org Q4_K_M + Q8_0 mmproj ~8.1 GiB, Q8_0 ~11.7 GiB): demoted, not dead. It is the one non-Qwen candidate that should load on the shipped b7972 pin (PR #18042 merged 2025-12-16, before the b7972 tag; confirmed in source, not run). KV is cheap (0.66 GiB). But the card admits it 'may overthink or repeat itself' and lists counting and person-identification limits, both relevant to a security verdict. It wants top_k 2 and repetition penalty 1.1, unlike our client defaults. Revisit only if an engine-unchanged non-Qwen A/B is wanted.
  - Cosmos family: Cosmos3-Edge is vLLM-only with BF16 the only tested precision. Its card recommends --max-model-len 131072, which is ~14 GiB of f16 KV for one sequence. INT4-AWQ is a TensorRT-Edge-LLM/Jetson artifact that does not run under vLLM, NF4 is a research artifact, and thinking is on by default. Cosmos-Reason2-8B looped under our JSON schema and was dropped by the owner. Cosmos3-Nano needs vLLM plus a plugin, or a single-author tensor re-key GGUF. Cosmos-Reason2-2B is gated and its card is unreadable.
  - Muse-Glimmer-30B (17GB build is 15.6 GiB of weights, no fit at 16 GB). Its json_schema fix (#29615) is only in builds from b11246 onward, so the pinned b11090 would ignore response_format.
  - Small models that fit trivially but waste the 16 GB tier: Qwen3.5-4B/2B, Gemma-4 E4B/E2B, MiniCPM-V-4.6 (0.8B LLM, too small for risk calibration), MiniCPM-V-4.5 (same Qwen3-8B backbone as the shipped model, no capacity gain, GGUF licence unresolved), and LFM2.5-VL-3B (non-OSI licence with a USD 10M revenue threshold, MMMU-Pro 30.5). Qwen3.5-4B is the natural 10 GB-tier pick, not a 16 GB one.
  - vLLM / NVFP4 / AWQ paths at 16 GB: Qwen3.5-9B AWQ is 8.5-11.6 GiB of weights and the 9B NVFP4 is 9.4 GB. vLLM is not shipped, VSS cannot run two vLLM engines per GPU, NVFP4 has FP4 tensor cores only on sm_120 (Ada falls back to Marlin W4A16), and BF16 embeddings make the quants larger than GGUF Q4_K_M (5.68 GB). GGUF is the VRAM-efficient single-box path.
  - MiMo-V2.6-Distill-Qwen-9B (Q8_0-only in ggml-org, 10.9-12.3 GiB): an agentic/coding/cyber SFT of Qwen3.5-9B with no perception evidence, 12 days old, and needing the #29319 template fix. Intern-Decision-4B emits calibrated probabilities but cannot describe, is HF-only, and is 7 days old; use it as a design reference for calibration, not a pick. Mage-VL and InternVideo3 are video/streaming watch items (mainline llama.cpp and vLLM PRs for Mage-VL were closed unmerged). Abliterated/'uncensored' mirrors were not considered (unvetted provenance for a security product).
- **VRAM math [C]:** Method [C]: total = weights + mmproj (real HF blob bytes / 2^30) + KV (2 slots x 16,384 = 32,768 cells, q8_0 = 34/32 B per element) + recurrent state + overhead. The formula is calibrated on our own A5500 data. Shipped 8B pair: 5.383 + 2.391 (2,448 MiB, matches the repo's measured KV line exactly) + 0.8 buffers + 0.77 resident detector/reid (9,606 - 8,822 MiB) = 9.34 GiB vs measured 9.38-9.60. The conservative 1.9 GiB overhead (1.12 buffers + 0.77 stack) is back-solved from the 4B stack at 7.02-7.06 GiB and gives 9.66 for the 8B (0.06-0.28 over measured). Budget is 0.85 x 16 = 13.6 GiB.\n\nPRIMARY Qwen3.5-9B (unsloth GGUF, mmproj-F16 918,166,080 B = 0.855 GiB). KV is 8 full-attn layers x 4 KV heads x 256 hd x 2 = 16,384 elements per token x 1.0625 B = 17,408 B/token x 32,768 = 0.531 GiB. DeltaNet state is 24 layers x ~2.2 MB (f32 assumed) x 2 slots = 0.098 GiB. Cache total is 0.629 GiB [C, state dtype not verified in llama.cpp].\n- Q6_K: 7,458,301,152 B = 6.946 GiB. Files 7.80 + cache 0.63 = 8.43; +0.8 buffers = 9.23 (VLM only); +0.77 stack = 10.0; conservative +1.9 = 10.3 GiB. Headroom 3.3.\n- Q8_0: 9,527,502,048 B = 8.873 GiB. Files 9.73 + 0.63 = 10.36; +0.8 = 11.16; +0.77 = 11.93; conservative 12.2 GiB. Headroom 1.4.\n- Q4_K_M: 5,680,522,464 B = 5.290 GiB. 6.14 + 0.63 = 6.77; conservative 8.7 GiB.\n\nFALLBACK Qwen3-VL-8B: Q4_K_M 4.682 + mmproj 0.701 + KV 2.391 = 7.77 + overhead; MEASURED whole stack 9.38-9.83 GiB (A5500) [R/E]. Q6_K 6.264 + 0.701 + 2.391 + 1.9 = 11.26; Q8_0 8.111 + 0.701 + 2.391 + 1.9 = 13.10 (12.0 VLM-only at 0.8) vs 13.6 budget. KV is 36 layers x 8 x 128 and is identical for 4B and 8B.\n\nAMBITIOUS Qwen3.8-27B IQ2_S: 9,259,510,912 B = 8.62 GiB + mmproj BF16 931,146,528 B = 0.867 + KV 16 layers x 4 x 256 x 2 x 1.0625 B x 32,768 = 1.06 + state 48 layers x 3.1 MiB x 2 = 0.29 -> 10.84 + 0.8 = 11.65 GiB (12.8-13.2 conservative). Gemma-4-12B QAT: 6.497 + 0.163 + KV q8_0 0.68 (SWA ring of ~2,560 cells) to 1.26 (6,144 cells; --swa-full would be ~5.6 GiB and must not be set) + 0.8 = 8.1-8.7 GiB, 9.5 with the conservative overhead.\n\nUncertainty [?]: the buffer constant is calibrated only on Qwen3-VL (151k vocab); Qwen3.5/Gemma have 248-262k vocab and different vision compute buffers. The estimates carry +/-0.5 GiB until a real llama-server 'projected' line and nvidia-smi peak exist on a 16 GB card. Verifiers' recomputes at 0.8 GiB buffers are ~1.1 GiB lower than the scouts' calibrated totals; the real number is in between. Decode bytes per token (S4 driver): shipped 5.03 GB; Q4_K_M 5.68 (1.13x); Q6_K 7.46 (1.48x); Q8_0 9.53 (1.9x). 16 GB cards span ~288 GB/s (4060 Ti 16 GB) to ~960 GB/s (5080) [A, from memory].
- **Measure first on our corpus:** Nothing above is a measured accuracy claim. Everything is fit and support. Before adopting, replay tierb-v0 (450 stills, declared truth) through the existing synthbench harness, which means adding models.py rows (models.yml has no VLM row). Run order: (0) CONTROL: re-run the shipped Qwen3-VL-8B Q4_K_M on the SAME newer llama.cpp build and flags (FA on/off, q8_0 KV) as the primary. This separates engine/flag effects from model effects and checks #29629 FA-embedding drift against the 36.1-36.5% S3 / 6.7-9.1% S2 baseline. (1) Qwen3.5-9B Q6_K, non-thinking, SAME prompt. Then Q8_0 and Q4_K_M as one-variable quant steps. (2) Only if S3 does not move: Qwen3.8-27B IQ2_S, then Gemma-4-12B QAT (with -ub >= image tokens and a soak test). For each, record S2 (benign false-alarm <= 5%), S3 (incident recall at/above band >= 90%), S5 (refusals/truncations at the shipped max_tokens=1024; the enforcement probe 5/5 and tool probe on that model with images; confirm no thinking leak and that json_schema is actually enforced), and S4 p95 <= 30 s on a REAL 16 GB card (bandwidth differs ~3x across 16 GB SKUs). Run S4 with 4-image calls at the 1280 cap, since Q8_0 and 27B decode are the likeliest breakers. Record S1 as the whole-stack nvidia-smi peak at 250 ms with both slots busy, target <= 13.6 GiB, plus llama-server's 'projected' and 'llama_kv_cache: size' lines and the vision-encoder buffer at the 1280 cap. Add a prompt/threshold control: ~63% of Qwen's incident scores sit below band by ~54 points and the flagship only reached 58.9%, so any gain must not be credited to the model family until prompt calibration is held constant. Also run a soak test (hours of image traffic, 2 slots, alternating text and image turns) to catch the hybrid-recurrent and FA crash classes (#25717, #27931, #29830) before any pick is called stable.
- **Open questions:**
  - No 16 GB card has been measured; the only measured card is the 24 GB A5500. The real S1 peak, the vision-encoder compute buffer at the 1280-token cap, and the Qwen3.5 hybrid-state allocation (f32 assumed) are unmeasured [?].
  - Which is the first llama.cpp build with qwen35 + vision that works with our flags (FA, q8_0 KV, 2 slots, json_schema)? Support starts between b8000 and b8200 (not bisected). b11090 predates later vision/structured-output fixes (#29773, #29615), and b11376 is current [?].
  - Does json_schema actually get enforced with images on qwen35 with thinking off, and does the response stay inside the 1,024-token budget? #20345 reports grammar not enforced with thinking on; the unsloth template defaults to thinking off but other GGUFs may not [?].
  - Is --image-max-tokens 1280 honoured for the Qwen3.5 mmproj (source says the qwen3vl_merger window is 8-4096 tokens), and is the mmproj graph different enough (no deepstack) to change per-image token counts or detail [?]?
  - Does the model family matter for S3 at all? Repo evidence says the miss is conservative scoring under the shipped verification prompt (8B 36.5% vs flagship 58.9%). A LoRA or prompt/calibration lever may dominate any model swap. Qwen3.5 LoRA-to-GGUF conversion was only fixed 2026-09-30 (#28324), after b11090, so train with HF/PEFT and merge before quantizing [?].
  - S4 on low-bandwidth 16 GB cards: does Q6_K stay under p95 30 s on a 4060 Ti 16 GB (288 GB/s [A]), and does Q8_0 stay under it on any 16 GB card? Hybrid models also lose prompt-cache reuse (#21831), which costs latency, not VRAM.
  - Is the open mrope warning (#28166) harmless noise or real corruption, and is the CUDA FA vision-embedding error (#29629) present on sm_89/sm_120? It also touches the shipped Qwen3-VL path, so rule it out before attributing the 36% S3 to the model.
  - mmproj is F16-only for Qwen3.5 (+0.15 GiB vs Q8_0). Is a self-quantized Q8_0 mmproj acceptable? Provenance is one hop (unsloth; ggml-org/Qwen3.5-9B-GGUF returned 401). Which GGUF repo and sha256 do we pin?
  - Does quant level (Q4_K_M/Q6_K/Q8_0, or 2-bit for the 27B) change risk-score calibration more than extraction accuracy? Vendor claims of <=1 point are on generic benchmarks, not ours [?].
  - Gemma-4-12B QAT: is the CUDA illegal-memory-access report (#24310, RTX 4080 SUPER 16 GB, stale-closed) reproducible on our build, and does it work at 2 slots with -ub raised from the shipped 512? What does the Google-hosted Gemma licence page add beyond the Apache-2.0 tag [?]?
  - Does 'GB' mean GiB for the 0.85 budget on a 16 GB card, given display and driver VRAM reserve? 13.6 GiB assumes the full 16 GiB is available.

### 24 GB card (budget 20.4 GiB)
