- **Primary:** google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it-qat-q4_0-gguf - Google QAT q4_0 GGUF (gemma-4-26B_q4_0-it.gguf 14,439,363,584 B) + gemma-4-26B-it-mmproj.gguf 1,194,828,160 B [E, verified bytes] - **16.8 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp, NOT the shipped b7972 (no gemma4 arch there) [E]. The in-repo b11090 image is not enough as is. It predates PR #29773 (2026-10-01, caps image tokens to n_ubatch for non-causal models) and the large-image GGML_ASSERT fix #28954. Use b11376 (2026-10-03) or newer, or b11090 with -ub >= 2048 [C]. The repo ships UBATCH_SIZE=512, so this must change. Rebuild for sm_86 (A5500) or sm_89 (4090); .env.example ships arch 89. No vLLM is needed. The vendor w4a16-ct checkpoint exists only for E2B/E4B/12B/31B, and the NVFP4 build is Blackwell-only [E].
  - Why: Chosen on fit and support, NOT on accuracy for our task [?]. (1) Fit: whole stack 16.8 GiB by formula, 17.4 GiB conservative [C], against the 20.4 GiB bar. That leaves 3.0-3.6 GiB headroom, the largest of any >=25B-class option that fits. Do NOT set --swa-full, which adds ~3.0 GiB and leaves ~0-0.6 GiB headroom [C]. (2) S4: only 3.8B active parameters [E card], so decode reads far fewer bytes per token than a dense 27B. It should land near or below the shipped 8B's p95 of 16.2 s [C], unmeasured. (3) The 4-bit is Google's own QAT checkpoint; the card claims bf16-like quality, which I did not verify [E/?]. (4) Thinking is off unless <|think|> is in the system prompt. With thinking off the 26B still emits an empty thought block, so the client must strip it [E card]. That avoids the Qwen3.5+ thinking-by-default S5 hazard. (5) Per-image token budgets of 70/140/280/560/1120 are a first-class knob, and llama.cpp clamps to 70..1120 [E clip.cpp]. Four images at 1120 is 4,480 tokens, within our 5,120 budget. (6) Apache-2.0, ungated, vendor MMMU-Pro 73.8, MATH-Vision 82.4, OmniDocBench 0.149 (vendor-reported, general VQA, not security) [E]. (7) Different lineage from Qwen, so it can separate 'Qwen is conservative' from 'any VLM is conservative' on our 62.7%-below-band pattern [?]. Honest caveat: Qwen's own table puts Qwen3.6-35B-A3B higher on perception rows (RealWorldQA 85.3 vs Gemma-26B 72.2, Qwen-measured, not comparable across vendors) [E]. It may win on our corpus, so replay both. Extra work: new llama.cpp pin and sm_86 rebuild; -ub 2048; re-run the S-2 json_schema enforcement probe (gemma4 issues #22396/#23990 closed not_planned, #23993 open); strip the empty thought block; soak-test 2 slots; add a models.py row; image before text per the card.
- **Fallback:** Qwen/Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct-GGUF - Q8_0 (8,709,519,456 B) + official mmproj Q8_0 (752,289,728 B), both from the same Qwen repo [E, verified bytes] - **12.8 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp b7972 as shipped, with the same client, prompt and schema path. S-2 json_schema is ENFORCED on this build and S5 was 0/13 unparseable [R]. No engine change, no vLLM.
  - Why: This is the shipped model at higher precision, the one-variable control for 'does precision or slots matter' versus 'does a different model matter'. (1) Fit: 12.8 GiB whole stack [C]. Cross-check: the measured shipped stack of 9.38-9.60 GiB plus the 3.43 GiB Q8_0 delta gives 12.8-13.0 GiB [R+C]. Headroom is about 7.4 GiB, which could buy 2 extra slots at 1.19 GiB each, the F16 mmproj (+0.38 GiB), or uncapped images. (2) Zero migration: same b7972 pin, same enforcement path, same prompt. (3) Honest limits: the S3 miss is calibration, not precision. 62.7% of incident scores sit below the declared band by 54 pts [R], and a 191.5 GB flagship still reached only 58.9% S3. Expect little S3 change from Q8_0 [?]. (4) S4 is not free: Q8_0 reads 1.73x the bytes per token of Q4_K_M (8.71/5.03 GB). If p95 were fully decode-bound it would scale 16.2 s to about 28 s, near the 30 s bar [C upper bound]. Prefill and vision do not scale, so the real figure is lower. It must be re-measured. The shipped Q4_K_M pair (9.4-9.6 GiB measured) remains the do-nothing baseline.
- **Ambitious:** Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B via unsloth/Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF UD-Q4_K_M (16,464,440,224 B) + mmproj-F16 (927,607,488 B). Whole stack is 19.1 GiB by formula and about 19.5 GiB conservative [C], leaving only 0.9-1.3 GiB of the 20.4 GiB budget, so keep 2 slots. UD-IQ4_XS (14,252,845,984 B) drops to about 17.1 GiB if headroom is needed [C]. Why ambitious: it has the strongest vendor vision card in this tier (RealWorldQA 85.9, OmniDocBench 91.1, CharXiv 83.7, vs Qwen3.6-27B 84.1/89.4/78.4; vendor-reported, thinking mode, not security) [E]. It is dense, so it is the simplest QLoRA base, and the public typed-decision adapters on this backbone (Cloudflare clef, autotrust JEV-27B-VL) show calibrated verdict heads are being built on it [E, vendor claims]. Risks: (1) S4: 16.5 GB read per token is about 3.3x the shipped 8B, so p95 near or over 30 s on an A5500-class card is plausible [C, unmeasured]. (2) Thinking is on by default with reasoning_effort defaulting to xhigh, so enable_thinking=false is mandatory against the 1,024-token budget [E]. (3) Needs llama.cpp newer than b7972; qwen35 is absent at b7972 and the first supporting build was not pinned [E]. (4) Hybrid-vision CUDA issues: #29629 (FA gives wrong mmproj embeddings), #28166 and #28877 (misaligned address on sm_120) [E]. (5) MTP/DFlash drafters exist as an S4 fix but have open bugs (#28158, #27282, #26894), so keep them off for the first run [E]. Alternate ambitious probe: Qwen3.6-35B-A3B UD-IQ4_XS, 18.6 GiB VLM-only and 19.4 GiB whole stack by formula (19.6-19.8 conservative) [C]. It is MoE, so S4 should be fast, and it has the highest vendor RealWorldQA (85.3), but it has 0.6-1.0 GiB headroom, 4-bit MoE quantization and the same Qwen3.5-family crash and thinking risks (#25717 stale-closed unfixed on our exact FA + q8_0 KV combination, #27931 open) [E]. Neither is vLLM-dependent: the vLLM builds do not fit 24 GB (Qwen3.8-27B INT4 is 18.12 GiB and AWQ 19.57 GiB of weights before KV and overhead; the NVFP4 builds are Blackwell-only) [E/C].
- **Rejected:**
  - google/gemma-4-31B-it QAT q4_0 (17.55 GiB files): 21.35 GiB VLM-only at -ub 2048 and 20.72 at -ub 512, so 21.5-22.1 GiB whole stack, over the 20.4 GiB bar [C]. It fits 32 GB. The only 24 GB variant is a 3-bit Q3_K_M of unmeasured quality [?]. It is also dense, so S4 reads about 3.5x the 8B bytes [C].
  - Qwen3.8-27B ggml-org Q4_K_M + mmproj Q8_0: 20.4 GiB VLM-only and 21.2 whole stack [C], over the bar. UD-Q4_K_XL is 19.4 GiB VLM-only and 20.1 whole stack, zero margin [C]. Only UD-Q4_K_M or UD-IQ4_XS is viable (see ambitious). Qwen3.6-35B-A3B has the same trap: ggml-org Q4_K_M is about 20.8 GiB VLM-only and UD-Q4_K_M about 22.7 GiB [C]. Only UD-IQ4_XS fits.
  - Meta Muse-Glimmer-30B 17GB Q4_K_M (18.0 GiB VLM-only, 18.8 whole stack, fits on paper) [C]: the card says reasoning cannot be turned off [E], which fights the 1,024-token S5 budget and S4. The response_format json_schema bug (#29613) was fixed only in b11246+, so b11090 ignores our schema [E]. Its default 4,096 visual tokens per image would fill a 16k slot with 4 images [C]. It is agent-tuned (OmniDocBench 75.8 vs Qwen3.8's 91.1, cross-vendor), and its usage policy restricts inferring identity or demographics of individuals, which needs a legal read for a person-centric product [E/?].
  - Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni-30B-A3B (NVIDIA lineage): GGUF Q4_K_M is 23.5 GiB VLM-only (24.3 whole stack) and UD-IQ4_XS about 21.4, neither fits [C]. vLLM NVFP4 is 20.87 GiB of weights plus 2-2.5 GiB runtime, and the card's minimum is an RTX 5090 32 GB [E]. On llama.cpp every image is a fixed 256 tokens (#25317 closed stale, tiling PR #23638 closed unmerged), so 1080p stills are under-resolved [E]. Revisit at 32/48 GB.
  - Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B (the only MoE that runs on b7972): Q4_K_M is 20.3 GiB VLM-only (21.1 whole stack), over the bar [C]. Q3_K_M (about 16.8 VLM-only, 17.5 whole stack) fits. But the repo saw 9/13 truncations and 10 refusals at the old 700-token budget [R], not re-measured at 1024, and the model is Sept 2025 with 3-bit calibration unknown [?]. Keep only as the b7972 contingency if the engine bump is refused.
  - Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct Q4_K_M (24.2 GiB VLM-only): GQA with 64 layers x 8 KV heads costs 4.25 GiB of q8_0 KV, so it misses [C]. Cosmos-Reason2-32B is the same weights class, also misses, and prescribes 4,096-token <think> output. The repo already saw Cosmos-Reason2-8B loop under the JSON schema [R].
  - Mistral-Small-3.2-24B (repo bake-off: 11/13 'uncertain', b7972 json_schema 500s, needs b11090) and Ministral-3-8B/14B: same lineage and hedging risk [R]; the 14B's own card advises ~1:1 aspect images, while ours are 16:9 [E]. Their only vision number is MM MTBench, which is a chat-quality metric [E]. Tool/json support is a vendor claim, untested.
  - InternVL3.5-14B and -30B-A3B: Aug 2025, not in llama.cpp's documented support list (InternVL 2.5/3 only), community GGUFs only [E]. The 30B-A3B Q4_K_M is 21.3 GiB whole stack and misses [C]. Dominated on 24 GB by the picks above.
  - Gemma-4-12B (any quant) as the 24 GB pick: fits easily (QAT 8.7 GiB VLM-only) [C] but is dominated by the 26B-A4B in the same family. It is the newest, least exercised gemma4uv projector path (CUDA illegal memory access #24310 on the official QAT q4_0 closed stale unfixed; #29386 sustained-image SIGKILL) [E]. Keep it for the 12/16 GB tiers.
  - Qwen3.5-9B Q8_0 (11.9 GiB whole stack) [C] and Qwen3.5-4B: fit but are the 12/16 GB-tier models. They leave over 8 GiB of the 24 GB tier unused and are beaten on the vendor card by the larger Qwen3.6/3.8. They also need the newer engine. Note that the unsloth Qwen3.5-9B GGUF template defaults to thinking OFF, unlike the card [E]; whether the Qwen3.8 GGUF templates do is not checked [?].
  - Security-labelled or NVIDIA-derived small fine-tunes: AnomalyThink-Qwen3-VL-8B (industrial MVTec-style, emits <think>), CamVLM-8B (gated, README unreadable, 9 downloads, BF16-only 19.5 GiB VLM-only, dataset CC BY-NC), HolmesVAU-2B and Vad-R1 (video-only, no GGUF, Vad-R1 unlicensed), Cosmos3-Nano/Edge (vLLM plugin or one-author GGUF, warehouse/ITS benchmarks), MiMo-V2.6-Distill-Qwen-9B (agentic/coding SFT, not perception) [E]. None targets home-security still verdicts or fits our b7972/llama.cpp contract.
  - Keye-VL-2.0-30B-A3B needs the vendor llama.cpp fork (a third engine pin), and Q4_K_M is 20.3-20.5 GiB VLM-only (21.1 whole stack), over the bar [E/C]. Qwen3.5-122B-A10B only fits 48 GB at 2-bit [C].
- **VRAM math [C]:** Budget: 0.85 x 24 = 20.4 GiB for the WHOLE stack (S1; the A5500 reports 24,564 MiB). Method [C]: whole stack = (GGUF + mmproj bytes)/2^30 + KV (2 slots x 16,384 = 32,768 cells, q8_0 = 34/32 B per element) + recurrent state for hybrids + 0.8 GiB buffers + 0.77 GiB detector/reid residents (repo-measured 784 MiB). Calibration check: the shipped 8B Q4_K_M by this formula is 4.683 + 0.701 + 2.391 + 0.8 = 8.575 GiB, vs a measured llama-server peak of 8,822 MiB = 8.615 GiB [R]. Adding 0.77 gives 9.35 vs 9.38-9.60 GiB measured whole stack, an under-count of 0.03-0.25 GiB [R]. The 0.8 GiB was calibrated on a 151k-vocab Qwen with a 1,280-token image cap. For the 248k-262k vocab families (Gemma, Qwen3.5+) the scouts used 1.2-1.5 GiB, so I show a conservative figure too. Vision compute buffers at 1,120-token images are unmeasured [?]. PRIMARY Gemma-4-26B-A4B QAT: weights 13.448 + mmproj 1.113 = 14.561 GiB. KV from config.json [E]: 25 sliding layers (8 KV heads x 256) and 5 global layers (2 KV heads x 512). Global is 5 x 2,048 el/tok x 32,768 x 1.0625 B = 0.332 GiB. Sliding is 25 x 4,096 el/tok x 3,072 ring cells ((1024 window + 512 ubatch) x 2 slots) x 1.0625 B = 0.311 GiB. KV = 0.643 GiB, assuming the default SWA cache and no --swa-full [C]; attention_k_eq_v might shave ~0.17 GiB if K=V sharing is implemented [?]. Then 14.561 + 0.643 + 0.8 = 16.00 GiB VLM-only, +0.77 = 16.8 GiB whole stack. Conservative (buffers 1.5, KV 0.95 at -ub 2048) is about 17.4 GiB. Headroom 3.0-3.6 GiB. With --swa-full the sliding KV becomes 3.32 GiB, so total about 19.8-20.4 GiB, which is marginal and should not be used. FALLBACK Qwen3-VL-8B Q8_0 + mmproj Q8_0: 8.111 + 0.701 = 8.812 GiB. KV = 36 layers x 8 KV heads x 128 x 2 = 73,728 el/tok x 1.0625 = 78,336 B/tok x 32,768 = 2,448 MiB = 2.391 GiB, which matches the repo-measured line exactly [R]. Then 8.812 + 2.391 + 0.8 = 12.00 GiB VLM-only, +0.77 = 12.8 GiB whole stack. Measured anchor: 9.38-9.60 GiB + 3.43 GiB (Q8_0 minus Q4_K_M bytes) = 12.8-13.0 GiB. AMBITIOUS Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q4_K_M: 15.334 + 0.864 = 16.198 GiB. KV = 16 full-attn layers x 4 KV heads x 256 x 2 = 32,768 el/tok x 1.0625 x 32,768 = 1.0625 GiB. DeltaNet state = 48 layers x about 3.1 MiB x 2 slots = 0.29 GiB [C, assumes fp32 state, not read from llama.cpp]. Then 16.198 + 1.0625 + 0.292 + 0.8 = 18.35 GiB, +0.77 = 19.1 GiB (19.5 with the scouts' larger buffers). Headroom 0.9-1.3 GiB. Ruled out by the same arithmetic (whole stack): Gemma-4-31B QAT 21.5-22.1; Qwen3.8-27B ggml-org Q4_K_M 21.2; Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B Q4_K_M 21.1; Qwen3-VL-32B Q4_K_M 24.9; Nemotron-Omni Q4_K_M 24.3 GiB. What spare VRAM buys at 24 GB [C]: for the hybrid and SWA models, KV is cheap (Gemma-26B about 0.32 GiB per slot), so spare buys slots, not detail, because Gemma's 1,120-token/image ceiling cannot be lifted. For Qwen it can buy a higher image cap (uncapped is about 2,040 tokens per 1080p still vs our 1,280 cap) or a higher quant. None of it is shown to raise S3: the same-prompt 8B to 191.5 GB flagship jump was 36.5% to 58.9%, still far from 90% [R].
- **Measure first on our corpus:** Nothing here is an accuracy claim; all fit numbers above are [C] until a real load is logged. Run on the A5500 (sm_86, the only measured card) through the existing synthbench harness. The harness MODELS list covers only qwen3-vl-8b, qwen3-vl-4b, nemotron-12b-vl, cosmos-reason2-8b and flagship, so each candidate needs a new models.py row, and models.yml has no VLM row to pin sha256.

(0) Fit gate per candidate, before any scoring. Record llama-server 'projected to use N MiB', the 'llama_kv_cache: size =' line (this settles Gemma's SWA accounting: 0.6 vs 3.65 GiB), and the whole-stack nvidia-smi peak with yolo26 + reid resident. S1 requires <= 20.4 GiB, all layers on GPU, and no --n-cpu-moe.

(1) Contract gate. Re-run the S-2 response_format json_schema enforcement probe with images and 2 slots. Verify thinking is off and strip Gemma's empty thought block. Re-check that the probe max_tokens budget does not truncate (finding G cliff). Then S5 on the 13-item set plus tierb-v0 at max_tokens 1024, counting unparseable, refusals and length truncations. Log refusals on weapon/intruder stills for Gemma specifically [?].

(2) S2/S3 replay on tierb-v0 (450 stills, ideal-detector rows). Run the Qwen3-VL-8B Q4_K_M control in the same session as the comparison baseline (36.1-36.5% S3, 6.7-9.1% S2 [R]). Report S3 and S2 with Wilson intervals, S3 by scenario, S2 on hard negatives (hooded_jogger, power_tools_at_night), and the fraction of incident scores below the declared band (control 62.7% by 54 pts). Treat a candidate as better only if its S3 interval clears the control's upper bound (42.8%). That decision rule is my proposal, not a repo rule.

(3) S4 on the shipped path: 21 requests at 4 images, cold and warm, p95 against 30 s (8B Q4_K_M baseline 16.2 s). Measure the dense Qwen3.8-27B explicitly (S4 is its likely failure) and the Q8_0 fallback (about 28 s if fully decode-bound [C]). For Gemma, compare image budgets 560 vs 1120 with -ub 2048. For Qwen, keep the 1,280 cap.

(4) Soak: at least 1 hour of 2-slot sustained image traffic, including 1920x1080 stills, to catch #29386 / #27931 / #29735-class failures.

(5) Confounder controls. Before attributing the 36% to the model, rule out llama.cpp issue #29629 (CUDA flash-attention gives wrong mmproj embeddings, growing with image size, seen on sm_86; also hits Qwen3-VL) by re-running a tierb-v0 subset with flash-attn off. Also hold the model fixed and vary only the prompt or thresholds, because 62.7% below band says the lever may be calibration. Only after that is a LoRA or calibrated-head comparison fair.

- **Open questions:**
  - Which exact llama.cpp build for Gemma 4? Support is absent at b7972 and present at b9000, with the first supporting build unbisected [E]. b11090 predates #29773 (2026-10-01) and the #28954 fix, so it needs -ub >= 2048 or a newer pin. Whether b11376 contains both is inferred from dates only [?].
  - Does json_schema enforcement hold on gemma4 with images and the empty thought block? #22396 and #23990 were closed not_planned (not fixed), #23993 is still open, and PR #21537 is unmerged [E]. Untested on our schema [?].
  - Gemma-4 SWA KV accounting in llama.cpp at 2 slots: 0.6 vs 3.65 GiB depending on --swa-full and the SWA cell count, plus whether attention_k_eq_v halves global KV [?]. The vision compute buffer at 1120-token images is also unmeasured (warmup is 256 tokens; #29735 reports a ~4 s reallocation on the first large image) [E/?].
  - Does Gemma 4 refuse or hedge on weapon and intruder imagery (S5)? Google's card claims low unjustified refusals, but nothing is measured on our corpus [E/?].
  - Is the S3 miss a model effect at all? 62.7% of incident scores sit below band by 54 pts, and a 191.5 GB flagship reached only 58.9% [R]. Prompt, threshold, a LoRA, or a calibrated typed-decision head (the Intern-Decision/JEV pattern) are untested levers. Public evidence on LoRA is mixed: Hawkeye reports +26.6 pp on 2.5K synthetic examples (in-distribution, self-reported), while the Qwen3.8-27B UCF-Crime LoRA shows all three seeds' dAUROC CIs spanning 0 and FP/h tripling [E].
  - Dense Qwen3.8-27B S4 p95 on an A5500-class card (about 3.3x the 8B's bytes per token), and whether MTP/DFlash drafters work with multimodal + json_schema on CUDA given open bugs #28158, #27282 and #26894 [C/?].
  - Qwen3.5-family CUDA hybrid-vision stability under our config (2 slots + FA + q8_0 KV + json_schema): #25717 is stale-closed unfixed on a similar matrix, #27931 is open [E]. Do the unsloth/ggml-org Qwen3.8 GGUF chat templates default thinking OFF, as the unsloth Qwen3.5-9B template does? [?]
  - No security-scene benchmark exists for any candidate. All vendor numbers (MMMU-Pro, RealWorldQA, OmniDocBench) are general VQA, vendor-run, and not comparable across vendors [E]. tierb-v0 is declared-truth with an ideal-detector assumption, so it is optimistic [R]. The only measured 24 GB card is the A5500; 3090/4090 bandwidth and S4 differ [?].
  - Licensing: Gemma 4 repos are tagged apache-2.0, but the card links Google's own Gemma 4 license page, which I did not read [E/?]. Muse-Glimmer's usage policy and Nemotron's NVIDIA Open Model Agreement terms were also not fully read.

### 32 GB card (budget 27.2 GiB)

- **Primary:** Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B - GGUF UD-Q6_K from unsloth/Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF (21,983,677,344 B) + mmproj-F16 (927,607,488 B). Step down to UD-Q5_K_M (19,771,509,664 B) only if the measured whole-stack peak exceeds 27.2 GiB. Avoid IQ-class quants (see the #28877 caveat). - **24.7 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp newer than the shipped b7972, which we do not ship yet. The qwen35 arch is absent at b7972 and present at b11090 [E]. The earliest supporting tag was not bisected [?], and the newest tag seen is b11376 (2026-10-03). Needs a fresh image built for sm_120 with CUDA >= 12.8 (the repo's existing b11090 image is sm_103; .env.example ships arch 89) [R]. Run llama-server with thinking off and re-run the S-2 enforcement probe.
  - Why: Qwen3.8-27B is the highest-capability model that fits 27.2 GiB with spare headroom. It is in the same vendor family and projector path as the shipped Qwen3-VL-8B. [E] Apache-2.0, ungated. Dense 27.8B hybrid (48 DeltaNet + 16 full-attention layers, 4 KV heads x 256 dim). ViT patch 16 / merge 2, the same 32 px/token arithmetic as Qwen3-VL, so the repo's 1280-token image cap should carry over [C]. The converter maps its mmproj to qwen3vl_merger (inferred; the mmproj header was not read) [C].

Vendor card (self-reported, general VQA, not security stills): RealWorldQA 85.9, OmniDocBench 91.1, CharXiv-RQ 83.7 [E]. Hybrid KV is cheap (1.06 GiB at 2x16k q8_0 versus 2.39 GiB for the shipped 8B) [C]. The budget therefore goes to weights. Q6_K fits with about 2.5 GiB spare. The only larger ggml-org Q8_0 build does not fit (about 29.4 GiB VLM-only).

Quantization is the weakest lever on vendor data. NVIDIA reports 0.4 points of mean loss for NVFP4 on Nemotron Omni, and Meta reports 0.2-1.0% for 4-bit Muse [E]. Neither is our model or our task, and quant effect on risk calibration is unmeasured [?]. I therefore spend spare VRAM on Q6_K plus image detail, not on a second model.

This is not an accuracy claim. Nothing here has been replayed on tierb-v0. The shipped 8B's S3 miss (36.1-36.5%) is mostly conservative scoring: 62.7% of incident scores sit below the declared band by 54.2 points, while objects are confirmed on 96-98% of stills. Even the roughly 191 GB flagship reached only 58.9% S3 and 8.6% S2 [R]. Treat the model swap as a secondary lever behind prompt, threshold and calibration work.

S4 is plausible but unmeasured [?]. Dense decode on a 22 GB file is bandwidth-bound. A vendor-measured dense 30B (17 GB) ran 74.9 tok/s on an RTX 5090 [E], which implies about 55-60 tok/s for this file [C]. The repo's longest 8B verdict was 852 tokens, which is about 15 s of decode plus prefill. The 5090's own bandwidth figure was not fetched [?].

Thinking is on by default (reasoning_effort defaults to xhigh, preserve_thinking on) [E]. The shipped client sends no enable_thinking [R]. It must be disabled server-side or the 1024-token budget turns into S5 truncation, the failure the 4B showed at 8/13 [R].

- **Fallback:** Qwen/Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct-GGUF - Q4_K_M + mmproj Q8_0 exactly as shipped (5,027,784,800 + 752,289,728 B). No-engine-change upgrade to Q8_0 + mmproj Q8_0 (8,709,519,456 + 752,289,728 B), which is 12.9 GiB whole stack [C] and unmeasured. - **9.6 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp b7972, the shipped pin: no engine change, same client and harness. Measured ENFORCED json_schema on this stack [R].
  - Why: This is the only candidate with S1-S5 measured on our stack [R]. The A5500 whole-stack peak is 9.38-9.60 GiB (9.83 uncapped), with 37/37 layers on GPU and S4 p95 16.2 s against the 30 s bar. S5 was 0/13 refusals on gen-2 and 1/450 on tierb-v0. tierb-v0 S3 is 36.1-36.5% and S2 is 6.7-9.1%, so it fails S3 and is marginal on S2 [R].

On a 32 GB card it leaves about 17.6 GiB idle. The no-risk one-variable upgrades, which keep the same engine, are Q8_0 weights and PARALLEL 4 (+2.39 GiB KV).

Q8_0 estimate [C]: 8.81 GiB files + 2.39 GiB KV + 0.87 GiB buffers (repo-measured 889 MiB) + 0.77 GiB residents = 12.84 GiB. Cross-check: shipped 9.38-9.60 plus the 3.43 GiB weight delta = 12.8-13.0 GiB.

It is the control for every other row, not a fix. Precision and slots will not move the calibration miss, because Qwen3-VL-4B and 8B share KV geometry and the misses are low scores, not missed objects [C]. It must also be re-run on whatever new build the primary needs, to separate an engine effect from a model effect [?]. Engine-unchanged size-step controls that also load on b7972 are listed under rejected.

- **Ambitious:** AMBITIOUS (needs a runtime we do not ship). nvidia/Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni-30B-A3B-Reasoning-NVFP4 on vLLM 0.20.0.

Why it is on the list:

- NVIDIA-authored, 31B total / ~3B active hybrid Mamba2/MoE; license 'other', NVIDIA Open Model Agreement, ungated [E].
- Its own card lists 'Minimum GPU NVFP4: 1x RTX 5090 32GB' [E]. NVFP4 is native on consumer Blackwell sm_120, which Ada and Ampere cards lack.
- NVIDIA reports NVFP4 within 0.4 points of BF16 on a 9-benchmark mean (non-reasoning). That is NVIDIA's own table, not our task [E].
- The Instruct recipe is max_tokens 1024, temp 0.2, top_k 1, with enable_thinking=false. That matches our 1024 verdict budget [E].
- On vLLM, image tokens are dynamic: 1,024-13,312 patches, about 256-3,328 tokens per image. Native video and audio help the later video phase [E].

VRAM [C]. NVFP4 weights are 22,409,034,192 B = 20.87 GiB [E]. KV is about 0.10 GiB (6 attention layers x 2 KV heads x 128 dim), plus about 0.09 GiB Mamba state. Add 0.8-2.5 GiB vLLM overhead (unmeasured, [?]) and 0.77 GiB residents. Total is about 22.6-25.5 GiB, at or under 27.2.

Extra work: a vLLM 0.20.0 image (CUDA 12.9 or 13.0), a client move from llama-server response_format to vLLM structured outputs, and a new enforcement probe. JSON-schema behaviour on this model is not documented [?]. The card also notes --moe-backend triton for a FlashInfer bug on RTX Pro [E]. A second engine breaks the repo's single-GPU llama.cpp shape [R]. Thinking is on by default.

Do not run it through llama.cpp. There the projector is fixed at 256 tokens per image (mtmd fixed_size preprocessor), regardless of resolution. Issue #25317 was stale-closed unfixed and tiling PR #23638 was closed unmerged [E]. Issue #27141 (nemotron_h_moe ssm_scan abort) is also still open [E].

Same-card alternative on the same new runtime: nvidia/Qwen3.8-27B-NVFP4, 21.92 GB. The vLLM total is about 26 GiB [C, 2.5 GiB overhead assumed], leaving about 1.2 GiB headroom. SGLang notes show Qwen3.8-27B measured on RTX 5090 and an SM120 NVFP4 GEMM [E].

Same-engine comparators, with no new runtime beyond the primary's llama.cpp bump:
(a) google/gemma-4-31B-it QAT q4_0. The files are 17.557 GiB and the VLM-only total is 21.35 GiB at -ub 2048, so about 22.1 GiB with residents [C]. It is a different training lineage, useful if Qwen's conservative scoring is lineage-specific [?]. It needs -ub >= 1120 because the repo ships 512, which would cap each image at 512 tokens (#29773) [E].
(b) Qwen/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B UD-Q4_K_M. VLM-only 22.71 GiB, about 23.5 GiB with residents [C]. MoE with ~3B active is the S4-safe option, but see the #25717 caveat under rejected.

- **Rejected:**
  - Qwen3.8-27B Q8_0 (ggml-org 28,595,763,648 B), UD-Q8_K_XL (31.46 GB) and official FP8 (30.39 GB): the VLM-only total is about 29.4-29.7 GiB for Q8_0, and the FP8 weights alone are 28.3 GiB. All exceed 27.2 GiB [C, summed from fetched bytes]. Q8_0 is also not what the vendor data says is worth buying.
  - Qwen3.6-35B-A3B at UD-Q6_K (29,308,320,736 B) and Q8_0 (36.9 GB): VLM-only 29.4 and 36.5 GiB [C], so they miss 27.2. UD-Q5_K_M was not sized [?]. UD-Q4_K_M fits (about 23.5 GiB with residents) and is kept only as a comparator. Qwen3.6-35B-A3B is not primary because it is the same qwen35moe architecture as llama.cpp issue #25717: CUDA illegal memory access on vision requests with flash-attn plus q8_0 KV on an RTX 5070 Ti (sm_120, Windows, b10002/b10016), worked around by FA off plus f16 KV, stale-closed unfixed [E]. Issue #27931 is open. One commenter reports zero crashes at 1,561 two-slot requests on an RTX 5090 [E]. A 4-bit MoE also has a quality-loss risk that is unmeasured [?].
  - google/gemma-4-31B-it at Q6_K (28.4 GiB VLM-only), Q8_0 (35.3) or --swa-full (about 46.9 GiB with KV 14.6): all miss 27.2 [C]. Q5_K_M is about 25.1 GiB [C] and fits but is thin. The QAT q4_0 build is retained as a comparator. Known risks: the n_ubatch >= image-tokens assert and the server capping images to n_ubatch (#29773), and Gemma-4 vision regressions through September [E].
  - Qwen3.5-122B-A10B: even UD-IQ1_M is 32.7 GiB (34.2 GB weights + 0.91 GB mmproj), so nothing fits at or below 27.2 GiB [E]. Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (180B) and the other 100B+ MoEs are far beyond any consumer tier [E].
  - Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni on llama.cpp (ggml-org Q4_K_M + mmproj Q8_0, about 23.5 GiB VLM-only, 24.3 with residents [C]): the projector is fixed at 256 tokens per image, so 1080p stills are squeezed. Issue #25317 was stale-closed unfixed, PR #23638 (tiling) was closed unmerged, and #27141 (ssm_scan abort at context reserve) is open [E]. Viable only through vLLM; see ambitious.
  - Meta Muse-Glimmer-30B (unsloth UD-Q5_K_M about 21 GiB, Meta Q4_K_XL about 20.7 GiB [C]): reasoning cannot be switched off on the GGUF template, which conflicts with the 1024-token budget (S4/S5) [E]. json_schema was ignored on b11090. It was fixed by PR #29615 (merged 2026-09-29), first present in tag b11246 [E]. It is agent/coding-oriented, with OmniDocBench 75.8 against 91.1 for Qwen3.8-27B in Qwen's table (cross-vendor harnesses disagree) [E]. USAGE_POLICY.md prohibits inferring private information about individuals, which needs legal review for person analysis [E].
  - Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct Q4_K_M (official GGUF; VLM-only 24.17 GiB, about 24.9 with residents [C]) and Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B Q4_K_M (VLM-only 20.34 GiB, about 21.1 with residents [C]): both load on the shipped b7972 (qwen3vl / qwen3vlmoe registered [E]). Kept as engine-unchanged size-ladder controls, not recommended. They are October 2025 models, and the 32B pays 4.25 GiB of q8_0 KV (8 KV heads x 64 layers). The 32B was never loaded in this repo. The official repo only offers Q4_K_M/Q8_0/F16, and Q8_0 is 38.2 GiB [C]. The 30B-A3B was measured here at 9/13 truncated at the old 700-token budget with 10 refusals, and was not re-measured at 1024 [R].
  - Cosmos-Reason2-32B (community Q4_K_M, about 24.2 GiB VLM-only [C]) and Cosmos3-Nano/Edge: the 8B sibling looped under JSON schema in this repo [R]. The 32B card prescribes <think> with max_tokens >= 4096 and tests BF16 only [E]. Its 'Smart Spaces' score is a single warehouse spatial-QA benchmark, not security. Cosmos3 needs vLLM plus a custom plugin and has no llama.cpp path; its GGUF is a one-author re-key [E].
  - InternVL3.5-38B (Q4_K_M 33.95 GiB VLM-only because the mmproj alone is 10.5 GiB, so it misses [C]); InternVL3.5-30B-A3B Q5_K_M (23.24 GiB [C]) and Keye-VL-2.0-30B-A3B Q4_K_M (about 20.3-20.5 GiB [C]): they fit but are not recommended. InternVL3.5 is not named in llama.cpp's documented-support list and is 13 months old [E]. Keye needs a vendor llama.cpp fork, a third engine pin [E].
  - Small models (Qwen3.5-4B/9B, Gemma-4-E4B/12B, MiniCPM-V-4.6): they fit with huge headroom but waste the tier. They are the 10-16 GB tier picks and are covered by the other tier scouts.
  - Avoid IQ-class quants of Qwen3.8-27B. llama.cpp issue #28877 reports a CUDA misaligned-address crash on Qwen3.8-27B IQ4_NL on an RTX 5090 Laptop (sm_120), still open [E]. K-quants such as UD-Q6_K and UD-Q5_K_M were not reported affected [?].
- **VRAM math [C]:** Budget: 0.85 x 32 = 27.2 GiB for the whole stack (S1: detector, specialists and VLM resident, no VLM offload). All GiB = bytes / 2^30. Totals marked [C] are computed, and no 32 GB card was measured.

PRIMARY (Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q6_K, 2 slots x 16,384 = 32,768 KV cells, q8_0 KV):

- Weights 21,983,677,344 B = 20.474 GiB [E bytes]. mmproj-F16 927,607,488 B = 0.864 GiB [E].
- KV from config.json [E]: 64 layers = 48 linear + 16 full-attention, 4 KV heads, head_dim 256. K+V is 16 x 2 x 4 x 256 = 32,768 elements/token. q8_0 is 34/32 B/element, giving 34,816 B/token x 32,768 = 1.0625 GiB [C].
- DeltaNet recurrent state: 48 layers x ~3.1 MiB x 2 slots = ~0.29 GiB [C, assumes f32 state; the llama.cpp allocator was not read].
- Subtotal 22.69 GiB. Add buffers and residents:
  - Verified on the task spec (0.8 GiB buffers): 23.49 GiB VLM-only, then +0.77 residents (repo-measured 784 MiB) = 24.26 GiB.
  - Scout basis (1.2 GiB buffers for the 248k vocab / hidden 5120, +0.77): 24.67 GiB. I carry 24.7 as the conservative total.
- Headroom against 27.2 GiB: about 2.3-2.9 GiB.
- Step-downs: UD-Q5_K_M (18.413 GiB) gives about 22.6 GiB whole stack. UD-Q4_K_M (15.33 GiB) gives about 19.5 GiB.
- If the sm_120 flash-attn vision crash precedent (#25717) forces f16 KV: +0.94 GiB, about 25.6 GiB, still fits [C].
- Lifting the image cap from 1280 to about 2,040 tokens/still: KV +~0.1 GiB, but the vision-encoder compute buffer is unmeasured [?].
- Calibration: the same recipe on the shipped 8B gives 9.38-9.67 GiB against 9.38-9.60 measured [C]. It is unvalidated for hybrid DeltaNet and a 248k vocab, so the first llama-server 'projected' line and nvidia-smi peak must replace it.

FALLBACK (shipped Qwen3-VL-8B): measured 9.38-9.60 GiB whole stack on the A5500, KV line 2,448 MiB [R]. Q8_0 upgrade [C]: 8.812 files + 2.391 KV + 0.87 buffers + 0.77 residents = 12.84 GiB.

COMPARATORS [C], VLM-only then +0.77 residents:

- Gemma-4-31B QAT q4_0: 16.439 + 1.118 + KV (2.37 at -ub 512, 2.99 at -ub 2048) + 0.8 = 21.35 GiB at -ub 2048, about 22.1 with residents.
- Qwen3.6-35B-A3B UD-Q4_K_M: 20.614 + 0.838 + 0.332 + 0.123 + 0.8 = 22.71 GiB, about 23.5 with residents.
- Nemotron Omni NVFP4 on vLLM: 20.87 + 0.19 + overhead (0.8-2.5) + 0.77 = 22.6-25.5 GiB.

Gaps: vision compute buffers at 1280 and ~2,040 tokens/image, the f32-state assumption, and Gemma's SWA cell count are unmeasured [?]. The shipped KV and total were measured only on 24 GB hardware [R].

- **Measure first on our corpus:** Nothing above is an accuracy claim. Every number is a fit or support statement. All candidates must be replayed on OUR corpus before adoption.

Setup: use the existing synthbench harness, which only replays rows in synthbench/run/models.py (qwen3-vl-8b, qwen3-vl-4b, nemotron-12b-vl, cosmos-reason2-8b, flagship) [R]. Add rows for Qwen3.8-27B, Gemma-4-31B and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B first. Use the same prompt, the same ideal-detector rows and the same 450 tierb-v0 stills. Note this makes tierb-v0 optimistic [R].

Order: 0. Fit gate on a real sm_120 32 GB card, because GB300 and A5500 numbers do not transfer [R]. Build llama.cpp for sm_120 (CUDA >= 12.8). With detector and reid resident, 2 slots x 16k and q8_0 KV, record the 'projected' line, the 'llama_kv_cache: size =' line (expect about 1.06 GiB plus state), all layers on GPU, and the nvidia-smi whole-stack peak against 27.2 GiB (S1).

1. Enforcement probe (S-2) with images and thinking off (--chat-template-kwargs enable_thinking=false or --reasoning off). Re-check the probe token budget (the 400-token cliff, finding G) and that the grammar holds under json_schema (#20345). Soak test 2 slots + FA + q8_0 KV + 4-image calls for the sm_120 crash precedents (#25717, #28877, #27931) and the mtmd FA embedding bug (#29629). Repeat with FA off / f16 KV as the workaround.
2. Replay all 450 stills and report S3 (bar >= 90%; baselines 36.1-36.5% shipped and 58.9% flagship), S2 (<= 5%; baselines 6.7-9.1%, with hard negatives hooded_jogger, power_tools_at_night and flashlight_neighbor reported separately), S5 (refusals and VlmTruncatedError at max_tokens 1024), and S4 p95 (bar 30 s; baseline 16.2 s) on 4-image calls at the 1280 cap and again at about 2,040 tokens/image. Also report the share of incident scores below the declared band (baseline 62.7%, mean gap 54.2 points). That separates a calibration gap from a size effect.
3. Controls. Run the shipped 8B Q4_K_M on the same box and the same new build, and on b7972, so an engine effect is not read as a model effect. Run one prompt/threshold control on the 8B, because the miss is calibration and a family or size comparison is confounded without it.
4. Then Q5_K_M versus Q6_K, f16 versus q8_0 KV, and Gemma-4-31B QAT (-ub >= 1120) and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B UD-Q4_K_M as same-engine comparators.
5. Only after that, the vLLM NVFP4 path (Nemotron Omni, Qwen3.8-27B NVFP4). It adds a second engine and needs its own S-2 probe.

- **Open questions:**
  - Which llama.cpp build first supports Qwen3.8 (qwen35) with working vision? Only b7972 (absent) and b11090 (present) were checked; the tag was not bisected [?]. The Qwen3.8 mmproj's projector type was inferred from converter mappings, not read from its header [?].
  - Does --image-max-tokens / LLAMA_ARG_IMAGE_MAX_TOKENS=1280 actually apply to the qwen35 mmproj? The clip.cpp QWEN3VL branch sets the limit and honours custom_image_max_tokens at source level [E], but it was never run [?]. Multi-image (4 stills per call) behaviour was not read [?].
  - Is the thinking default OFF or ON in the unsloth Qwen3.8 GGUF chat template? For the unsloth Qwen3.5-9B GGUF the embedded template defaults to thinking OFF, the opposite of the base card [E]. The Qwen3.8 GGUF template was not checked [?].
  - The real buffer term for a 27B with a 248k vocab: 0.8 GiB (spec) or 1.2 GiB or more (scout) [?]. The vision compute buffer at the 1280 cap and at about 2,040 tokens/image, the f32 recurrent-state size, and whether q8_0 V-cache is safe on hybrid layers are all unmeasured [?].
  - RTX 5090 memory bandwidth was not fetched, so the S4 estimate rests on one vendor-measured tok/s number for a different dense model [?]. Llama.cpp prefix-cache reuse degrades on hybrid recurrent models (#26994, #21831), which costs latency, not VRAM [E].
  - Does quantization (Q6_K versus Q4) change risk-score calibration or schema fidelity? No evidence exists for our task. Vendor loss numbers are general-benchmark and for other models [?].
  - Is the Blackwell-only advantage (native NVFP4) worth a second engine? NVIDIA lists the RTX 5090 for Nemotron Omni NVFP4. For Qwen3.8-27B NVFP4 the SGLang notes show SM120 work. vLLM JSON-schema behaviour on either is undocumented [?], and NVFP4 on consumer sm_120 under vLLM is otherwise unverified [?].
  - Fine-tuning path: llama.cpp LoRA-to-GGUF for Qwen3.5-family V-head reordering was only fixed on 2026-09-30 (#28324), after b11090 [E]. The only public LoRA on Qwen3.8-27B found (UCF-Crime shoplifting, weak labels) shows AUROC +0.02-0.03 with all three seeds' CIs including 0, and false positives/hour tripled (29 to 92) [E, self-reported]. Do not assume a LoRA fixes calibration. Whether QLoRA of a 27B base fits 32 GB is unmeasured [?].
  - Corpus caveat: tierb-v0 labels are declared-truth on FLUX.2 stills with ideal-detector rows, and the handoff's 1280x720 versus 1920x1080 inconsistency is unresolved [R]. Licenses: Qwen3.8, Gemma-4 and Muse are apache-2.0 on the tags [E], but Gemma's license page and Muse's usage policy were not legally reviewed [?].

### 48 GB card (budget 40.8 GiB)

- **Primary:** Qwen/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B - GGUF UD-Q6_K (unsloth/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-GGUF, 29,308,320,736 B [E]) + mmproj-BF16 (902,822,624 B [E]). Optional A/B arm: Q8_0 (36,903,140,320 B [E]). - **30.2 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp, a build at or above the repo's already-built second pin b11090 (ai-vlm:sm103-b11090; newest release seen b11376). NOT b7972: arch qwen35moe is absent at b7972 and present at b11090 [E, source grep]. The true first-supporting build was not bisected [?]. The mmproj reuses the existing qwen3vl projector, with --image-max-tokens honoured in clip.cpp source [E, not run]. Needs an image rebuilt for the card's SM (sm_86 / sm_89 / sm_120); the repo only has sm_86 and sm_103 images.
  - Why: Chosen on fit, S4 risk and lineage, NOT on measured accuracy; nothing here is a claim that it scores better on our task [?]. Why this one: (1) At 48 GB, latency (S4) rather than VRAM is the discriminator. This is a ~3B-active MoE (from the A3B name [C]), so decode streams about 2.5-3 GB per token, versus about 22 GB for dense Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q6_K and 5.0 GB for the shipped 8B Q4_K_M [C]. The shipped 8B's p95 of 16.2 s on the A5500 leaves headroom against the 30 s S4 bar [R]. (2) Hybrid attention: only 10 of 40 layers hold a KV cache (2 KV heads x 256 head_dim, from config.json [E]). KV is 0.33 GiB at 2 slots x 16k q8_0, against 2.39 GiB for the shipped 8B [C]. An extra 16k slot costs about 0.23 GiB, so spare VRAM buys slots and a higher image-token cap, not just bits. (3) Same vendor and same vision geometry as the shipped model (patch 16, merge 2), so the client, prompt, schema and 1280-token cap carry over unchanged for a clean A/B [C]. (4) Vendor-reported, not ours: RealWorldQA 85.3, HallusionBench 69.8, MMMU-Pro 75.3, MMBench-EN 92.8 [E]. Public-benchmark returns from more capacity are small: the Qwen3.5 card has 122B-A10B vs 35B-A3B at MMMU 83.9 vs 81.4 and RealWorldQA 85.1 vs 84.1, with HallusionBench 67.6 vs 67.9 [E]. The repo's own size ladder, 8B 36.5% to flagship 58.9% S3, is still far from 90% [R]. Do not spend the 48 GB on a 2-bit 122B. UD-Q6_K leaves about 10 GiB; spend it on slots and image tokens first. Q8_0 is an A/B arm only: it fits at 36.5-37.9 GiB, but only 2.9 GiB is left once the cap is lifted. RISKS: (a) second engine pin plus a rebuild per SM. (b) Thinking is ON by default [E card]; the shipped client and Dockerfile send nothing to disable it [R]. A thinking run is an S5 truncation at max_tokens 1024, and grammar enforcement was not applied with thinking on (llama.cpp #20345, closed). Set server-side --reasoning off or chat_template_kwargs enable_thinking=false, then re-run the enforcement probe. (c) Hybrid-recurrent + M-RoPE vision on CUDA has open reports: #25717 (illegal memory access with FA + q8_0 KV on sm_120, closed stale and unfixed, on the 35B-A3B), #27931 (open), #28166 (position warnings on every image) and #29629 (CUDA FA wrong mmproj embeddings, which also touches the shipped Qwen3-VL). Our PARALLEL 2 + FA + q8_0 KV + images is inside that matrix, so a soak test is mandatory [E]. (d) Recurrent state defeats prompt-cache reuse (latency, not VRAM) [E].
- **Fallback:** Qwen/Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct - GGUF Q8_0 (Qwen/Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct-GGUF, 32,483,932,992 B [E]) + mmproj Q8_0 (712,148,928 B [E]). Zero-new-variable alternative: the exact Q4_K_M file already run here (18,556,687,168 B [E]), 20.34 GiB VLM-only [C]. - **34.1 GiB**
  - Runtime: llama.cpp b7972, the shipped pin, with no engine change. Arch qwen3vlmoe is registered in both b7972 and b11090 [E, source grep]. Same qwen3vl projector, same client, non-thinking Instruct. Needs a models.py row to replay on tierb-v0; it ran only in the older n=13 bake-off harness [R].
  - Why: This is the largest model that runs on the shipped engine, which makes it the closest 48 GB-class candidate to what is already proven on our stack. (1) It was loaded here on b7972 at Q4_K_M. Its only 'ruled out' reason was fit on 24 GB at f16 KV: broker actual 22,688 MiB against a 20,889 MiB bar [R]. That reason disappears at 48 GB. (2) Instruct (non-thinking), so there is no thinking-budget hazard. MoE with ~3B active keeps S4 close to the 8B: n=13 gave median 6.7 s and p95 23.9 s, but the p95 was inflated by truncated runs [R]. (3) HONEST CAVEAT: it is not 'proven'. The only evidence is the n=13 bake-off at a 700-token budget: 9 of 13 truncated, 10 refusals, S3 0.25 with 6 of 8 refused [R]. The budget is now 1024, so that result is not a verdict on today's shipped shape [?], and the enforcement probe was not separately run for the MoE variant [?]. The Qwen3.6 evidence is no stronger, because nothing new has run on tierb-v0 yet. (4) It is an older generation (2025-09; repos untouched since 2025-11) and is outscored by Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on public vision tables [E, vendor]. It is kept for engine risk, not for expected quality. (5) If it also fails S5 at 1024, the real fallback is the shipped Qwen3-VL-8B (Q4_K_M or Q8_0) unchanged. That control is mandatory in the replay anyway.
- **Ambitious:** Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B (dense, newest Qwen open VLM; GGUF unsloth/Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF UD-Q6_K 21,983,677,344 B + mmproj-BF16 931,146,432 B = 23.5 GiB VLM-only by the formula [C], about 24.3 whole-stack, 24.9 on the scouts' conservative constant; Q8_0 from ggml-org 28,595,763,648 B = 29.65 [C]). Same b11090+ engine requirement as the primary. Case for it: strongest vendor vision table in the tier (RealWorldQA 85.9, OmniDocBench 91.1, CharXiv-RQ 83.7 [E, vendor and agentic-heavy]); hybrid KV is cheap (1.06 GiB q8_0 plus 0.29 GiB recurrent state [C]); and a dense 27B is the simplest QLoRA base for the owner's fine-tune question. LoRA caveats: MoE LoRA is more fragile; GGUF conversion of Qwen3.5-family LoRAs only got fixed by llama.cpp #28324 on 2026-09-30, after b11090, so plan on merge-then-quantize [E]. Since the S3 miss looks like score calibration (62.7% of Qwen incident scores sit 54 pts below band [R]), a calibration-targeted LoRA is the lever to test later, not a bigger model. Warning from the public record: the one published Qwen3.8-27B UCF-Crime LoRA gave dAUROC +0.02 to +0.03 with all three seed CIs including 0, and FP/h on normals rose 29 to 92. Weak-label LoRA can raise sensitivity while multiplying false alarms, against our 5% S2 bar [E, self-reported]. Cost and risk: S4 is the likely breaker. Dense decode streams about 22 GB per token (4.4x the shipped 8B's bytes) on a card whose bandwidth we did not fetch, so p95 under 30 s is unproven [C, pessimistic upper bound]. Thinking is on by default with reasoning_effort defaulting to xhigh, so it must be disabled explicitly. Keep the MTP/DFlash speculative drafters OFF in the first run: open llama.cpp bugs (#28158, #27282, #26894, #27833) make them a confounder [E].
- **Rejected:**
  - Qwen3.5-122B-A10B (unsloth UD-IQ2_M 39,148,942,528 B + mmproj-F16 908,724,960 B): the only >100B model that squeezes in, at 38.8 GiB formula (about 39.6 whole-stack, 1.2 GiB spare against 40.8) [C]. Rejected: 2-bit quality on any risk-calibration task is unmeasured and no card has 2-bit data [?]; the gain over 35B-A3B on vendor tables is 1-2.5 points and HallusionBench is flat or worse (67.6 vs 67.9) [E]; same hybrid-vision llama.cpp risks; UD-Q2_K_XL (41.31 GiB) does not fit. Capacity is not the S3 lever here.
  - nvidia/Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni-30B-A3B (NVFP4 / FP8 / ggml-org GGUF): rejected for the first round, kept as a later NVIDIA-lineage look. (a) On llama.cpp every image is encoded at a fixed 256 tokens; mtmd routes nemotron_v2_vl to the fixed-size preprocessor, #25317 was closed stale and unfixed, and tiling PR #23638 was closed unmerged [E]. A 1080p still would be squeezed to one 512 px tile, which defeats the point of a 48 GB card. (b) Full resolution needs vLLM 0.20.0, a second engine; the repo's VSS cannot run two vLLM engines on a GPU [R]. FP8 is about 33.8 GiB formula and the vendor lists the L40S 48GB as the minimum GPU [E]. NVFP4 targets Blackwell (RTX 5090 32GB) and gives nothing on Ada/Ampere 48 GB cards [E]. (c) #27141 (nemotron_h_moe ssm_scan abort at context reserve) is open [E]. (d) Reasoning is on by default, the license is the NVIDIA Open Model Agreement (text not read) [?], and the Q8_0 GGUF is 33.9 GiB formula with no accuracy upside.
  - Muse-Glimmer-30B (meta-models; unsloth/official K-quant GGUFs): rejected. (a) b11090 ignores response_format json_schema for this arch; the fix (#29615) merged 2026-09-29, after b11090, and first appears at b11246 [E], so a newer pin is forced just to get schema output. (b) Reasoning cannot be turned off, only strength-tuned, which is a direct S4/S5 hazard at max_tokens 1024; the thinking-tag PR #27475 is unmerged [E]. (c) Meta's USAGE_POLICY prohibits inferring sensitive information about individuals, which needs legal review for a home-security product [E]. (d) It trails Qwen3.8-27B on Qwen's own OmniDocBench/OSWorld rows, and the two vendors' harnesses disagree [E]. (e) Agent-tuned, with no perception or security evidence.
  - Gemma-4-31B-it (QAT q4_0 or Q8_0) and Gemma-4-26B-A4B as primary: not rejected as arms, but not recommended first. Gemma-4-31B is dense, so it has the same S4 risk as Qwen3.8-27B with no evidence it is better on our task. The QAT q4_0 build is 21.35 GiB and Q8_0 is 35.3 GiB at -ub 2048 [C]. Its KV is the heaviest of the field, and --swa-full pushes the total to about 46.9 GiB [C]. The image-budget ceiling is 1,120 tokens, and the server caps image tokens to n_ubatch for non-causal Gemma4 (#29773). The repo ships UBATCH_SIZE=512, so images are capped to 512 tokens or hit the assert (#21427, #28954 open on b11090). Gemma's llama.cpp vision path has had regressions as late as 2026-09-04 [E].
  - Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct dense (official GGUF): rejected. 64 layers x 8 KV heads makes KV 4.25 GiB q8_0, so Q8_0 is 38.2 GiB formula (about 39 whole-stack, about 1.8 GiB spare) [C]. Q8_0 streams 34.8 GB per token, 6.9x the shipped 8B. It is the oldest generation, was never loaded here at 32B, and is dominated by Qwen3.8-27B (KV 1.06 GiB) [E/C].
  - Cosmos-Reason2-32B and Cosmos3-Nano / Cosmos3-Edge: rejected for now. The repo already measured Cosmos-Reason2-8B looping under the JSON schema (the owner dropped it 2026-09-30) [R]. The 32B card prescribes a <think> format and max_tokens of at least 4096, which collides with the 1024-token and 30 s contract [E]. Its 'Smart Spaces' score is a single warehouse spatial-QA set (77.79 vs 47.55), not security [E]. The Cosmos3 official path is vLLM plus a custom plugin, with only community GGUF extractions and a BF16-only 'tested' statement [E].
  - InternVL3.5-38B / 30B-A3B and Keye-VL-2.0-30B-A3B: rejected. InternVL3.5 is not in llama.cpp's documented support list (only InternVL 2.5/3) and has community GGUFs only. The 38B mmproj alone is 10.5 GiB (6B ViT), giving 33.95 GiB formula for a model about 13 months old [E/C]. Keye needs a vendor llama.cpp fork, a third engine pin, with H800-oriented kernels [E].
  - vLLM FP8 / NVFP4 / AWQ serving for the primary or ambitious picks (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B FP8 is 37.49 GB; Qwen3.8-27B FP8 about 30.9 GB): rejected as the first path. It adds a second engine and loses the llama.cpp json_schema/grammar path already proven in the repo. FP8 is about 38 GiB for the 35B-A3B, leaving thin headroom [C]. NVFP4 has no FP4 tensor cores on Ada/Ampere 48 GB cards (RTX 6000 Ada, A6000, L40S) and falls back to weight-only Marlin; native FP4 exists only on Blackwell (RTX PRO) and is unmeasured by us [E/?]. GGUF is the VRAM-efficient single-box artifact.
  - Not on the table (all [E]): Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 180B (the repo's 'flagship' identity, 123.6 GiB NVFP4), GLM-4.6V 106B at 1.6-bit (quality collapse risk), Llama-4-Scout at 2-bit (gated, old, restrictive license), Kimi-K3 / DeepSeek-V4 / GLM-5.3-Flash (hundreds of GB). Also unvetted: community abliterated/'uncensored' repacks. They are tempting for S5 but have unreviewed provenance and altered safety behaviour for a security product.
- **VRAM math [C]:** Budget = 0.85 x 48 = 40.8 GiB [spec S1]. Method [C]: total = (GGUF + mmproj bytes) / 2^30 + KV at 2 slots x 16,384 = 32,768 cells, q8_0 (34/32 B per element) + recurrent state (fp32 assumed) + 0.8 GiB buffers (the task formula). Whole-stack adds 0.78 GiB for the measured detector/reid residents (9,606 - 8,822 MiB [R]). The scouts used a larger constant (1.5 buffers + 0.7 stack = 2.2 GiB), so I show 'formula' and 'conservative'. Calibration: the recipe reproduces the repo's shipped 8B llama-server (8.57 GiB formula vs 8,822 MiB = 8.62 measured), the repo's 30B-A3B at f16 KV (22,472 vs 22,688 MiB, -1.0%) and the whole-stack 9.38-9.61 GiB. The hybrid, MoE and recurrent terms, and vision compute at a higher token cap, are UNMEASURED [?]. All sizes are [E] HF tree bytes; conversions are mine.
  PRIMARY Qwen3.6-35B-A3B UD-Q6_K: weights 27.296 GiB + mmproj-BF16 0.841 + KV 0.332 (10 attention layers x 2 KV heads x 256 hd x 2 (K,V) = 10,240 elem/token = 10,880 B/token at q8_0 x 32,768; config.json [E]) + DeltaNet state 0.123 (30 layers x about 2 MiB x 2 slots) + buffers 0.8 = 29.39 GiB VLM-only. Whole-stack 30.17, conservative 30.79. Spare 10.0-11.4 GiB. The Q8_0 arm (34.37 GiB weights): 36.46, 37.24 whole-stack, 37.86 conservative. Spare about 2.9 GiB, thin if the image cap is lifted.
  FALLBACK Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B Q8_0: weights 30.253 + mmproj Q8_0 0.663 + KV 1.594 (48 layers x 4 x 128 x 2 = 52,224 B/token q8_0 x 32,768 = 1,632 MiB, matching the repo's measured 3,072 MiB f16 x 0.53125) + 0.8 = 33.31; whole-stack 34.09; conservative about 34.8 (scout, F16 mmproj). The exact Q4_K_M file the repo ran: 17.282 + 0.663 + 1.594 + 0.8 = 20.34.
  AMBITIOUS Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q6_K: 20.474 + mmproj-BF16 0.867 + KV 1.0625 (16 layers x 4 x 256) + state 0.292 + 0.8 = 23.50; whole-stack 24.28; conservative 24.90. Q8_0: 26.63 + 0.867 + ... = 29.65; whole-stack 30.43; conservative 31.05.
  OTHER ARMS, same method: Gemma-4-26B-A4B QAT q4_0 (14,439,363,584 + 1,194,828,160 B = 14.56 GiB files) is about 16.0-16.3 formula (KV 0.64-0.95 GiB if the SWA cache is not --swa-full), 19.0 with --swa-full; Q8_0 is 27.57 [C]. Gemma-4-31B Q8_0 is 35.3 at -ub 2048 [C]. Qwen3-VL-32B Q8_0 is 38.2; the whole stack of 39.0 leaves 1.8 GiB spare. Qwen3.5-122B-A10B IQ2_M is 38.8; the whole stack of 39.6 leaves 1.2 GiB spare. Nemotron Omni Q8_0 GGUF is 33.9 [C].
  Sensitivities: if '16k per slot' means 16,384 total across both slots, KV halves. A higher image cap raises vision compute buffers (unmeasured) but adds almost no KV on the hybrids: +5,120 image tokens cost about 52 MiB at q8_0 on the Qwen3.6 geometry [C]. The spare ~10 GiB therefore buys slots (about 0.23 GiB each), an uncapped image budget (about 2,040 tokens for a 1080p still), or Q8_0, in that order of value.
- **Measure first on our corpus:** Nothing above is an accuracy claim; vendor tables are general VQA and none is a security benchmark [E]. Before adopting, replay on OUR corpus (tierb-v0, 450 stills, declared truth; harness synthbench/run/models.py, which needs new rows for Qwen3.6-35B-A3B and Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, since only qwen3-vl-8b / 4b / nemotron-12b-vl / cosmos / flagship are replayable [R]), on the real 48 GB card (the repo has only ever measured a 24 GB A5500 [R]), in this order:

0. Load smoke test per model on the pinned build. Record llama-server's 'llama_kv_cache: size =' and 'projected to use' lines and the nvidia-smi peak; confirm all experts sit on GPU (no --n-cpu-moe or offload); confirm thinking is off (no <think> or reasoning_content leak). Re-run the S-2 enforcement probe and check that max_tokens 1024 does not trip the probe budget cliff (ledger finding G) [R].
1. S5 first, since it is cheapest and kills fastest: the 13 gen-2 items, then 450 stills at max_tokens 1024 and the 25 s read timeout. Pass = refusals about 0 and zero truncations; the baseline is 1 length truncation in 450 for the shipped 8B [R].
2. S1: whole-stack peak with detector + reid + VLM resident, both slots busy, against 40.8 GiB. Validate the 30.2 / 30.8 GiB estimate and the hybrid state and buffer terms. Then re-measure with the image cap lifted from 1280.
3. S2/S3 on all 450 stills with the SAME prompt and the same ideal-detector rows, two runs each. The shipped 8B moved 36.5 to 36.1 between runs and about 3/20 on the older set, so report CIs and per-scenario splits. Baselines to beat or explain: 8B S3 36.1-36.5% [30.3-42.8], S2 6.7-9.1%, 62.7% of incident scores below band by 54.2 pts, flagship S3 58.9% / S2 8.6%. Weak scenarios are child_alone_at_pool 0%, package_theft 0%, fire_or_smoke and knife_visible 52.6%. Hard-negative S2 leaks are hooded_jogger, power_tools_at_night and flashlight_neighbor.
4. S4: p95 over at least 21 warm requests plus cold and wake first-request latency, with 4 images at the 1280 cap and again uncapped. The dense Qwen3.8-27B is where S4 is most likely to fail (bar 30 s; the shipped 8B measured p95 16.2 s on a 24 GB card [R]).
   Controls: (a) re-run the shipped Qwen3-VL-8B on the same new build, to separate a model effect from a build effect. Issue #29629 (CUDA FA vision embeddings) can touch Qwen3-VL too. (b) Run a prompt or threshold control, because the S3 miss is conservative scoring, not missed objects (96-98% object confirmation): do not credit a model family with a gain until the prompt and calibration confound is ruled out.
   Optional third arm for lineage diversity: Gemma-4-26B-A4B QAT q4_0 at about 16 GiB. It needs -ub at least the image-token budget (the repo's 512 would cap images), thinking handling for the empty thought block, and a json_schema probe on gemma4 [E/?].
   Adopt only if S5 passes, S1 is at most 40.8 GiB on the real card, S4 p95 is at most 30 s, and S2/S3 move materially against the baselines above.

- **Open questions:**
  - Qwen3.6 thinking default in the GGUF chat template [?]: the card says 'will think by default', but the embedded unsloth Qwen3.5-9B GGUF template is inverted (thinking OFF by default) [E]. The Qwen3.6 GGUF template was not read; check it, and set --reasoning off or enable_thinking=false server-side regardless.
  - Minimum llama.cpp build for qwen35moe plus mmproj on CUDA was not bisected [?]. b11090 is chosen only because the repo already builds it; the newest tag is b11376. Does json_schema enforcement hold on qwen35moe with images and thinking off? [?]
  - Is q8_0 KV + flash-attn + 2 slots + images stable on qwen35moe? #25717 (stale-closed, unfixed), #27931 (open), #28166 and #29629 say no guarantee [E]. A soak test across the three arch variants (sm_86, sm_89, sm_120) is needed.
  - Is the 0.8 / 1.0 GiB buffer constant right for hybrid/MoE archs and for a raised image cap? It is calibrated only on Qwen3-VL 4B/8B/30B-A3B [C]. The recurrent-state size (fp32) is an assumption.
  - Which 48 GB card? RTX 6000 Ada and L40S are sm_89, the A6000 is sm_86, and RTX PRO is sm_120. Memory bandwidth (S4) was not fetched for any of them [?]; FP4 exists only on the Blackwell part. The repo's one measured card is the sm_86 A5500, so S4 numbers do not transfer.
  - Does the shipped client need an enable_thinking / chat_template_kwargs path, or is a server flag enough? Also the Qwen3.6 card's presence_penalty 1.5 recommendation versus schema-constrained JSON [?].
  - Does a higher image-token cap (about 2,040 tokens per 1080p still) change S2/S3? Qwen-family warns below 1024 tokens for grounding [E]. This is the one knob where spare VRAM could plausibly matter, but it is untested.
  - How much of the S3 gap can a calibration LoRA close, and on which base (dense 27B vs MoE)? The only public analogue, a Qwen3.8-27B UCF-Crime LoRA, was inconclusive and tripled false alarms [E]. Intern-Decision-4B (calibrated typed decisions, Brier/ECE, on Qwen3.5-4B) is a design reference only.
  - Existing security benchmarks: none found that matches our still-image verdict + risk-score task (UCF-Crime, XD-Violence, HIVAU-70k, TAR and Simuletic are video or detection) [E]. Several are non-commercial. 'Harbor' appears only as a generic agent-evaluation harness, not a VLM benchmark, and the repo has no VLM-LoRA or Harbor material [E/R]. tierb-v0 stays the primary benchmark.
  - Licensing and gating were read from HF tags only. Qwen3.6, Qwen3.8 and Qwen3-VL are Apache-2.0 and ungated [E]. The Nemotron agreement text and the Gemma license page were not read [?].

## 4. Benchmarks, Harbor, and fine-tuning (three research tracks)

### EXISTING SECURITY / SURVEILLANCE BENCHMARKS AND DATASETS (HF datasets API, arXiv, GitHub, official project pages; fetche

**Recommendation.** Q1 (do we need a benchmark, an existing one, then LoRA or train our own?): No existing public benchmark measures S2/S3 as you define them (single still, detector-gated, benign vs incident with a risk band and a verdict schema), so build and keep your own acceptance suite; public sets can only add real-footage cross-checks. Every incident-rich real set is video, most are research/non-commercial or unlicensed (CUVA NC-SA, UBnormal NC-ND, NWPU academic-only + no derivation, MSAD DUA forbids training surveillance models, UCA research-only, UCF-Crime/XD-Violence third-party YouTube/LiveLeak/movie footage with no stated terms; HF 'CC0/MIT' mirror tags are uploader-asserted). The commercially clean real option is MEVA (CC BY 4.0, 1080p fixed cameras, plentiful benign background plus person_steals_object and person_abandons_package). Plan: (1) keep tierb-v0 as the S2/S3 yardstick; (2) add a real-footage probe: MEVA benign + threat slice as the main real set, UCF-Crime test (150 normal + 140 in-interval anomalous videos, one frame per video) as an internal eval-only breadth check after legal review; (3) use MME-RealWorld Monitoring (1,601 stills) as a perception-regression guard for any LoRA; (4) report cluster-aware CIs (150 benign videos gives about +/-3.5 pts at 5%; 140 incident videos about +/-5 pts at 90%). Evidence on fine-tuning from this track: generic surveillance LoRA gives small gains (SurveillanceVQA Qwen2.5-VL-3B +5.4%, none on detection/classification), binary UCF-Crime detection is already ~0.90 for untuned Qwen2.5-VL-7B (so it cannot discriminate models), and the large public win (Qwen3-VL-8B 46.0 -> 75.6 on CCTV-Anomaly classification) needed 13k in-domain videos, RL, and a closed taxonomy. Your measured failure (S3 36% with ~97% object confirmation, scores ~53 pts under band) is calibration of the 0-100 band, which no public data teaches. Therefore: first try rubric/prompt + score-calibration fixes (including probability-weighted risk scoring over score bins, per arXiv 2608.21244: +7.7 to +20 pts on AD metrics), then LoRA on your own declared-band labels (tierb plus hand-verified real stills), not on public data; training a model from scratch is not supported by anything found. Q2/Q3 are outside this track.

**Smallest next step.** Eval-only real-footage probe (no training, no redistribution; get legal OK before touching UCF-Crime): (a) define a 4-6 row class->declared-band table (e.g., Shooting/Explosion/Arson high; Burglary/Robbery/Assault/Fighting mid-high; Vandalism/Stealing/Shoplifting mid; Normal benign) and write it down before looking at model output; (b) pull the 290 UCF-Crime test videos only (backseollgi/UCF-Crime_TEST_SET, ~15.8 GB, or per-file from mteb/ucf-crime) and take 1 frame per video: 150 from Testing_Normal, 140 at the midpoint of annotation_start1/end1 (frames); (c) run the existing detector gate + vlm_assess unchanged and report S2 (false alarms on 150 benign) and verdict=confirmed / score>=band-floor on 140 incident stills with Wilson CIs, tagged real-v0 and never mixed into tierb-v0; (d) in parallel, list a few annotated MEVA hours from the GitLab meva-data-repo and S3 bucket (metadata first) and count steals/abandons intervals to decide if a 1080p MEVA slice supports a real S3 estimate. If S2 on real normals diverges from tierb-v0's 6.7-9.1% by more than the CI, the synthetic corpus is mis-calibrated for S2.

**Findings.**

- **UCF-Crime (original; the de-facto real-footage reference)** - [E] 1,900 untrimmed real CCTV-style videos, 128 h, 13 anomaly classes (Abuse, Arrest, Arson, Assault, Burglary, Explosion, Fighting, Road Accident, Robbery, Shooting, Shoplifting, Stealing, Vandalism) + Normal. 950 anomalous + 950 normal. Official split: train 1,610 (800 normal/810 anomalous), test 290 (150 normal/140 anomalous). Collected from YouTube and LiveLeak by text search; paper itself calls the videos 'low resolution'. Media = VIDEO. Labels = video-level class; temporal anomaly interval(s) exist ONLY for the 140 anomalous test videos. [E] HF mteb/ucf-crime parquet (rows fetched via datasets-server): 290 test rows, columns category + annotation_start1/end1/start2/end2 (frame indices, -1 = none; e.g. Abuse028 = 165-240). All 140 anomalous test videos have interval 1; 16 have a second interval. Test class mix [E]: Normal 150, RoadAccidents 23, Shooting 23, Explosion 21, Shoplifting 21, Burglary 13, Arson 9, Arrest 5, Fighting 5, Robbery 5, Stealing 5, Vandalism 5, Assault 3, Abuse 2. S2/S3 expressibility: S2 yes only coarsely (the 150 test 'Normal' videos = 'no crime occurred'; stills from them are real benign). S3 only as 'frame inside annotated interval counts as incident'; NO severity or risk band anywhere, so our 'at/above declared band' notion must be imposed by us via a hand class->band map [C]. Frame-level label noise: a single still inside an interval of Stealing/Shoplifting/Burglary/Arrest may show nothing incriminating [C]. Power [C]: n=150 benign videos at S2=5% gives 95% CI about +/-3.5 pts (SE=sqrt(.05\*.95/150)=1.8 pts); n=140 incident videos at S3=90% gives about +/-5 pts, at 36% about +/-8 pts; frames from one video are correlated so the cluster count is videos, not frames. Obtain: not gated. HF mteb/ucf-crime = 1,900 videos, 103.8 GB tree sum + annotation parquet; backseollgi/UCF-Crime_TEST_SET usedStorage 15.8 GB (README shows only a tar.gz; that it is exactly the 290 test videos is [?]). Mean video ~54.6 MB [C: 103.8 GB/1,900]. (license: NO license or terms text on the official CRCV page (Wayback 2024 capture checked) [E-absence]. Footage is third-party YouTube/LiveLeak content [E]. HF/Kaggle mirrors self-assert CC0 (jinmang2/ucf_crime, hibana2077/UCF-Crime-Dataset), Apache-2.0 (ThePolyMath), or nothing (mteb, backseollgi) -- uploader-asserted, NOT authoritative. Treat as research/internal-eval only; legal sign-off before any commercial training use or redistribution [C]. | fit: ADAPT, eval-only. Best breadth of real incident classes and the only widely used real benign set with an official test split; usable for stills by sampling 1 frame/video in-interval vs normal. Not as-is: no severity, low-res vs our 1920x1080 stills, small per-class n, public-web videos may sit in VLM pretraining data [?].)
  - Evidence: https://arxiv.org/html/1801.04264 (collection, split, Table 2, 'low resolution'); https://export.arxiv.org/api/query?id_list=1801.04264; https://huggingface.co/datasets/mteb/ucf-crime (card + tree sum 103.8 GB); https://datasets-server.huggingface.co/rows?dataset=mteb/ucf-crime&config=default&split=test (290 rows, class counts); https://web.archive.org/web/2024/https://www.crcv.ucf.edu/projects/real-world/ (class definitions, 'Normal Event: no crime occurred'); https://huggingface.co/api/datasets/backseollgi/UCF-Crime_TEST_SET
- **UCA (UCF-Crime Annotation) and UCF-Crime-derived text sets** - [E] Human event-level sentence annotations on UCF-Crime: 1,854 videos, 23,542 sentences (avg 20 words), 110.7 h per the UCA paper (SurveillanceVQA Table 1 lists UCA at 121.9 h: unexplained discrepancy), start/end times to 0.1 s; json + txt on GitHub. Text/temporal grounding only; no severity, no risk label. Annotation form cannot express S2/S3; it supplies ground-truth descriptions for judge-scored 'describe' quality of the vlm_assess narrative [C]. (license: GitHub README: 'exclusively available for academic and research purposes' and tags Apache-2.0 [E]; videos are UCF-Crime with its unstated terms. Net: research-only in practice [C]. | fit: NOT USEFUL for S2/S3; marginal for narrative quality.)
  - Evidence: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.13925 (abstract via export.arxiv.org); https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Xuange923/Surveillance-Video-Understanding/main/README.md (counts, license text)
- **CUVA (Causation Understanding of Video Anomaly, CVPR 2024)** - [E] 1,000 real videos, 10 major categories (the HF classification prompt lists 11) and 42 subcategories, 32.5 h, mean 117 s. Three human annotation sets: what (type, start/end, description), why (cause), how (effect). 'How severe' = a per-second 'importance curve' (temporal severity trend), the closest thing to a severity annotation in any public set found [E]. HF fesvhtr/CUVA: 6,230 'full' rows + 1,200 'test' rows over 6 tasks (Detection, Classification, Timestamp, Description, Cause, Result) [E]; so test about 200 videos [C: 1,200/6]. Detection task asks 'is there an anomaly: output 1', and every sampled row I saw is anomalous, so benign/S2 coverage is probably absent [?]. Severity is a temporal curve over a video, not a per-still 0-100 score or declared band [C]. Videos crawled from Bilibili and YouTube. Obtain: not gated; 4 zips = 25.6 GB [C: 7.57+7.62+7.72+2.73 GB]; supported in lmms-eval as task 'cuva_test' [E README]. (license: CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 (GitHub README); HF card says 'unknown'; paper footnote: permission from Bilibili/YouTube 'for non-commercial purposes' [E]. NON-COMMERCIAL + ShareAlike. | fit: NOT USABLE commercially; reference only for how a severity annotation is shaped.)
  - Evidence: https://arxiv.org/html/2405.00181 (counts, importance curve, Bilibili/YouTube footnote); https://huggingface.co/datasets/fesvhtr/CUVA (+/tree/main/raw sizes); https://datasets-server.huggingface.co/size?dataset=fesvhtr/CUVA; https://raw.githubusercontent.com/fesvhtr/CUVA/main/README.md (lmms-eval, license)
- **HIVAU-70k (Holmes-VAU, CVPR 2025) -- hierarchical anomaly understanding** - [E] 70k+ multi-granular text annotations: 5,443 videos, 11,076 events, 55,806 clips built from the UCF-Crime and XD-Violence TRAIN sets; test = 3,300 samples (2,200 clip / 732 event / 398 video) from their TEST sets. Annotation form (range-fetched sample): per video 'judgement' (An anomaly exists, specifically named X), 'description', 'analysis', event intervals in seconds, per-clip captions; no severity. Official repo does not host videos (download from the UCF/XD homepages). HolmesVAU-2B (InternVL2-2B) needs under 9 GB GPU per README. HF mirrors: backseollgi/HIVAU-70k_UCF-crime 124.5 GB, \_XD-Violence 328.9 GB (no license). (license: GitHub repo MIT [E]; annotations inherit UCF-Crime/XD-Violence video terms (unstated / movies+YouTube) [C]. Mirrors carry no license. | fit: ADAPT at most: its judgement labels plus event intervals are a cleaner machine-readable UCF/XD interval source than raw UCF files; free-text outputs need an LLM judge. Cannot express risk bands.)
  - Evidence: https://arxiv.org/html/2412.06171 (5,443/11,076/55,806; 3,300 test); https://raw.githubusercontent.com/pipixin321/HolmesVAU/master/README.md (data layout, <9GB); https://api.github.com/repos/pipixin321/HolmesVAU (MIT); range fetch of https://huggingface.co/datasets/backseollgi/HIVAU-70k_UCF-crime/resolve/main/ucf_database_test.json and merge_instruction_test_final.jsonl
