**Accounting.** REPO VRAM ACCOUNTING (how it is summed). Budget rule: discrete cards <= 0.85 x card memory with 'GB' treated as GiB (24 GB -> 20.4 GiB = 20,889 MiB broker cap in the bake-off report; sizing.md rule cited at 08-audit-profile-anatomy.md:410-412; spec S1 at gaming-gpu-profile-design.md:83). S1 as worded = peak VRAM with detector + specialists + VLM resident and no CPU offload of the VLM, measured only on 24 GB-class hardware (F13; GB300 numbers are 'indicative', spec :83,:387). Shipped residency set is YOLO26 + reid (+ threat only if GATEWAY_ENABLE_THREAT) via Triton; face models run on CPU onnxruntime (0 VRAM), so spec S1's 'specialists resident' vs the shipped set must be settled before claiming S1 (handoff :125-128). WHAT IS SUMMED, measured: nvidia-smi sampler at 250 ms across a replay with both slots busy; whole stack = ai-gateway (yolo26+reid, tritonserver 676 MiB) + ai-vlm (llama-server peak 8822 MiB) + backend (0 MiB, CPU torch) + compositor 17-21 MiB = 9606 MiB (ledger item 39); full-slot ceiling 9,722-9,826 MiB (item 40). Non-VLM overhead therefore ~784 MiB [C: 9606-8822]. llama.cpp's own 'projected to use N MiB' = GPU weights + KV + compute buffers and EXCLUDES the mmproj and CUDA context: 8B on A5500 4455.34 + 2448 + 312.75 = 7216.09 -> '7216 MiB' [C, matches]; actual server peak 8822 = projected + 1606 MiB, of which mmproj Q8_0 is 717 MiB (752,289,728 B) and ~889 MiB is context + vision encoder buffer [C]. GB300 'broker vram_actual_mib' (11216 at f16 KV; 9,056 at q8_0 KV) and the 'projected' line are single-container floors on a 249 GiB GPU, never S1. Weights: summed from real GGUF byte counts + mmproj (8B pair 5.38 GiB, 4B pair 2.75 GiB, 30B-A3B 17.95 GiB, Mistral-24B 14.17 GiB [C: bytes / 2^30]); the repo documents a ~40% error from the parameter-count/VSS formula for NVFP4 (04:40-44; 05:133-136: formula understated the 12B-VL NVFP4 checkpoint by 43% because the vision tower stays BF16) - blob sizes are the rule. KV: llama-server's own line is the authority. Qwen3-VL 4B and 8B share block_count 36 / head_count 32 / ctx 262144 (only feed_forward_length differs 9728 vs 12288), so KV is identical: f16 4608 MiB for the WHOLE 2x16384 pool = 147,456 B/token [C: 4608 MiB / 32768 cells] = 36 layers x 4096 B -> consistent with 8 KV heads x 128 head_dim, x2 tensors x2 B (the repo prints head_count 32, which is query heads; the 8x128 split is my inference [C]); q8_0 = 34/32 B per element, so 4608 x 0.53125 = 2448 MiB [C, matches the measured line exactly]. Older prose '~73 KB/token, ~2.4 GB' was the q8_0 number; the f16 4608 was what the bake-off logged because ai/vlm/Dockerfile never passed CACHE_TYPE_K/V until commit 58163f23f (compose :222-223 now q8_0; Dockerfile :116-127). Resident-set at idle fell 11604 -> 9376 MiB. Other KV points: Nemotron-12B 768 MiB f16 (6 of 63 layers) [C: 408 MiB at q8_0]; 30B-A3B 3072 MiB f16 (48 layers) [C: 1632]; Mistral-24B 5120 MiB f16 [C: 2720]. WARNING for any new sizing: those candidate 'actual' numbers were all taken with f16 KV at CTX 32768/PARALLEL 2 and bake-off shape (before the q8_0 fix), so they overstate today's shipped shape by 40-47% of the KV pool. Per-image cost: LLAMA_ARG_IMAGE_MAX_TOKENS=1280 (client constant, pinned); uncapped 1.9-2.4 MP stills cost ~8,509 tokens for four (vs 5,003 capped); a 1920x1080 still smoke test = 1,245 prompt tokens. Text tokens: repo counter undercounts by 1.35x (Qwen splits digits), fit now budgets 1.5x. Per-slot window 16,384 with 1,024 output reserved; longest 8B verdict 852 tokens. Spec's pre-measurement VRAM table (spec :122-126: 12B-VL Q4 7.5 + mmproj 1.7 + KV/buffers ~2 + YOLO/Triton ~1 + specialists 1-2 = ~13-14 GiB) is a [C/A] estimate, never measured; the measured 8B stack is 9.4-9.8 GiB. models.yml has NO serving-VLM row (the pin lives in ledger item 35 and the Brev checklist; sha256 mechanism only covers face/reid rows) - verified by reading the whole file; MODELS list (replayable): qwen3-vl-8b, qwen3-vl-4b, nemotron-12b-vl (ai-vlm), cosmos-reason2-8b, flagship (vllm) only; the 30B-A3B and Mistral-24B ran under the older gen-2 n=13 bake-off harness and are NOT replayable on tierb-v0 without adding rows (models.py:36-97). Nothing in the repo mentions VLM LoRA/fine-tuning or 'Harbor' (grep of docs/, synthbench/, backend/evaluation: only image/video-generation LoRAs, unrelated); the only calibration evidence is that Qwen's S3 misses are low scores not rejections (62.7% below band by 54 pts) - relevant to the owner's fine-tune question but untested.

**Runtime constraints.** llama.cpp b7972 vs b11090 [all R, repo-read]: shipped pin is ARG LLAMA_CPP_REF=b7972 (ai/vlm/Dockerfile:46; image tags ai-vlm:sm103-v12 = d723137f4ed4 3.59 GB, ai-vlm:sm103-b11090 = a0fcc4267d5a 3.63 GB, ai-vlm:sm86 for the A5500 = b7972-e06088da0). b7972 loads and enforces json_schema on multimodal requests for qwen3vl (S-2 ENFORCED; nvext.guided_json silently IGNORED, so only native response_format works). b7972 CANNOT load Nemotron's mmproj (unknown projector type nemotron_v2_vl) -> b11090 (b11090-b1c2863e2, 2026-09-21) required for nemotron_h and, separately, for the Mistral GGUF because b7972's server-side response_format->grammar translation 500s at ~75-100% on that file; b11090 8/8 clean; the commit that fixed it was not chased. Registered archs in libllama.so of sm103-v12 (not the executable): qwen3vl, qwen3vlmoe, nemotron_h, mistral3. Both pins compile for sm_103 with -DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=103 (nvcc 13.3.73, aarch64; CUDA 13.3.1 base images; libgomp.so.1 must be mounted for the runtime base). ggml gates its Blackwell tensor-core MMA path on **CUDA_ARCH** >= 1200 (sm_120) for every pin through HEAD b11165; GB300 (cc 10.3) runs the Ampere/Turing MMA + cuBLAS paths (Dockerfile:39-45), so GB300 latency says nothing about 50-series speed. .env.example:598 ships CUDA arch 89; an sm_86 box must rebuild with 86 or it silently runs the wrong binary. Ampere (A5500) has no FP8/FP4 tensor cores (D2), so the A5500 proves fit and salience, not NVFP4 speed. FP4: NVFP4 on sm_120 is UNVERIFIED (04 §5); VLLM_USE_NVFP4_CT_EMULATIONS cannot rescue Ada (compressed-tensors path only); VSS's pinned NVIDIA-internal vLLM build flags cannot be read statically; spec 2.3.2 plans a startup-log reading on an RTX PRO 4500 (not run). vLLM on sm_103: the flagship and Cosmos run on the GB300 under vllm/vllm-openai nightly-aarch64 (0.28.1rc1.dev681+ge7edf17ce, 22.4 GB image); research note lists upstream vLLM sm_103 issues #30245 and #32647 and a Triton sm_103 gap (docs/research/cosmos-b300-docker-research.md:86-119,248-249 - not re-checked); VSS RT-VLM needs RTVI_VLLM_ATTENTION_BACKEND=TRITON_ATTN on GB300 (08:212,291), open whether sm_120 needs it (08:846). VSS structurally cannot run two vLLM engines on one GPU (dev-profile.sh:1161-1165, hard-errors at 48 GB L40S) - which is why one VL model + llama.cpp is the single-GPU shape (04:98-112). vLLM models with a reasoning/looping habit need request_extra/system_message (Cosmos) or enable_thinking=false (flagship). GATING/provenance: Qwen3-VL-4B/8B/30B-A3B official GGUFs apache-2.0 ungated (8B GGUF header has no license key; repo tag is the source); Nemotron nvidia-open-model-license, community GGUF; Cosmos-Reason2-8B gated:auto license 'other'; Mistral only via unsloth mirror (two provenance hops). Licenses are not a selection criterion for specialists (F12 owner ruling) - that ruling is about specialists only; I did not find a VLM-level equivalent [?]. OTHER HARNESS CONSTRAINTS that bite any new model: (1) enforcement-probe budget cliff (ledger finding G, item 28): the client probe caches ENFORCED once per client and sat at a 400-token budget that truncated break_in_attempt 3/4 (A) or 8/8 (B); verbose models can be refused by the probe, not the model; later \_ASSESS_MAX_TOKENS=1024 and probe +64 landed (2fd5c54bc, 7b2918580) - unclear if cliff closed for new models [?]; (2) max_tokens 1024 + 25 s read timeout + 90 s wake timeout are shipped constants; a model whose verdict runs >1024 tokens becomes an S5 failure (4B: 8/13; 30B-A3B: 9/13 at the old 700); (3) GGUF files fetched into the agent-gpu models root land mode 640 and the serving uid is 'other' -> chmod 644 or --user 0; (4) agent-gpu endpoint is host.docker.internal:18100 not 8098; (5) with no detections in the prompt Qwen3-VL-8B and the flagship answer 'uncertain, risk 0' (A6) - the corpus must carry detection rows (ideal-detector assumption makes tierb-v0 optimistic); (6) llama-server ignores SIGTERM ~10 s on stop; (7) 'synthbench replay' refuses in the sandbox because systemctl is absent (renderer-state check unreadable); driver bypass was owner-directed and records renderer_check: SKIPPED in run.json; (8) all S2/S3 results are declared-truth, stills from FLUX.2; handoff says corpus renders are 1280x720 while exported stills are 1920x1080 JPEGs - inconsistent with the 'FLUX.2 1920x1080' framing and not resolved [?]; item 40's older 38-item set used 1280x704 stills (~880 vision tokens, under the 1280 cap).

**Tier notes already in the docs.** WHAT THE DOCS ALREADY SAY ABOUT TIERS (R). 05-hardware-profiles.md:55-68 (marked superseded in part by errata E1,E3,E4,E6,E9,E10,E17,E18): Halo 32 GB (27.2 GiB): 12B-VL NVFP4 13.78 + RT-CV 3.0 = 16.78, fits with 10.4 GB spare; Volume 12-16 GB (10.2-13.6): 'small CV zoo -> text -> small text LLM'; Entry 8 GB (6.8): detection + alerting only, reasoning remote. 05:61-68 claims below ~24 GB 'a single large VL model is not viable' - a pre-measurement [C]/inference about the 12B NVFP4 blob (13.78 GB > the 16 GB budget of 13.6), not about 4B/8B Q4_K_M GGUFs, and it is overtaken by the A5500 whole-stack 9.4-9.8 GiB measurement. 05:147-155 open Q4 'does the product floor sit at 12 GB?' - unanswered. 04:84-96: 5090 (27.2) fits 12B NVFP4+RT-CV; 4090 (20.4) fits VRAM but FP4 would be emulated on sm_89; any VLM + local text LLM structurally blocked by the two-vLLM-engines rule. Spec (gaming-gpu-profile-design.md): the product target is ONE 24 GB card (A5500, sm_86, :56); S1 only measured on 24 GB-class (A5500 or Brev A10G/L4, :83); Brev matrix (:463-471, checklist) = A10G 24 GB sm_86, L4 24 GB sm_89, RTX PRO 4500 32 GB sm_120 [A], T4 16 GB (deliberate no-fit case); that matrix is owner-run and has NOT run; the 5090, 10, 12 and 48 GB cards appear in no measurement and no VLM-selection doc (48 GB appears only as the L40S where VSS hard-errors on two vLLM engines, and as the GB300's ~47.9 GiB free slice, which is not a 48 GB card tier). The checklist's T4 'does not fit' arithmetic (checklist :26-35,177-181) uses the f16 4608 MiB KV and is STALE: with q8_0 KV the 4B stack peaked 7.02-7.06 GiB on the A5500 [C: would fit the 13.6 GiB 16 GB budget; unmeasured on a 16 GB card]. ONLY MEASURED CARD: RTX A5500 24 GB (24564 MiB) sm_86: 8B stack 9.38-9.60 GiB (bar 20.4), 4B 7.02-7.06 GiB. INFERENCES FOR THE OWNER'S TIER QUESTION [C, all from repo numbers above, none measured on those cards; budgets 0.85 x card: 10 GB 8.5, 12 GB 10.2, 16 GB 13.6, 24 GB 20.4, 32 GB 27.2, 48 GB 40.8 GiB]: (a) 8B Q4_K_M at 2x16K q8_0: 9.4-9.8 GiB -> clears 12 GB (10.2, thin 0.4-0.8 GiB), 16 GB, and above; misses 10 GB (8.5). Halving to PARALLEL 1 / 16K saves 1224 MiB of q8_0 KV -> ~8.2-8.6 GiB [C], marginal at 10 GB; (b) 4B at 2x16K: 7.0-7.2 GiB fits 10 GB with ~1.3 GiB headroom but fails S5 at the shipped 1024-token budget (8/13 truncated); (c) Nemotron-12B Q4_K_M: 10118 MiB single-container at f16 KV + ~0.78 GiB stack ~ 10.7 GiB (about 10.3 GiB at q8_0 KV 408 MiB) -> misses 12 GB (10.2), fits 16 GB; (d) Mistral-24B: 20274-20680 MiB at f16 KV; swapping to q8_0 saves 2400 MiB and adding ~784 MiB stack gives ~18.2-18.6 GiB -> plausibly fits 24 GB (20.4) with ~1.8-2.2 GiB headroom, but it hedges 11/13 uncertain and needs b11090 - untested at q8_0; (e) 30B-A3B: 22688 - 1440 + 784 = 22032 MiB (21.5 GiB) -> still over 24 GB (20.4); fits 32 GB (27.2) and 48 GB (40.8) with a 700->1024 budget re-test needed; (f) Cosmos-Reason2-8B BF16 (16.33 GiB weights; 24,938 MiB at vLLM util 0.10) -> not 24 GB; 32/48 GB only, needs <think> prompt and 4096 tokens/120 s; (g) flagship NVFP4 ~191.5 GB resident: irrelevant to consumer tiers. ON 'MORE RESOURCES = MORE ACCURATE SCORING': the repo's only same-prompt, same-corpus size comparison is Qwen3-VL-8B (9.4-9.8 GiB stack) vs the 191.5 GB flagship: S3 36.5 -> 58.9%, S2 6.7 -> 8.6% - bigger gets more recall at slightly worse false alarms, but still far under 90% S3 and over 5% S2; survey on gen-2 n=13 (30B-A3B, 24B) showed no visible quality gain and failures on the budget/hedging axis, and n=13 cannot order models (bake-off report :45-62). The shared Qwen3-VL-4B/8B KV geometry means more VRAM only buys more slots/context, not scoring. The misses are mostly conservative scores (62.7% below band) under a verification-style prompt, so prompt/calibration/threshold levers are untested confounders to any model-size or LoRA comparison. DOCS/MATERIAL NOT READ IN FULL (unverified): docs/vss-integration/14-specialist-model-research.md (grep only; specialists, not VLMs); 11-errata-2026-09-23.md (only rows E13-E18, E27 read; E1,E3,E4,E6,E8-E10 not read); 02-model-inventory.md, 03-open-questions.md, 08/10 (grep hits only); ledger rows for G0.x/0.25/P0 and Phase 1 (1.1-1.7 headings only), rows 20-33 and 36-38 (headings/skims), rows 42-74 (CI/R8/main-green, grep-skimmed, not VLM-relevant); spec sections 3-4, 6-7 and §8 beyond the lines cited; the full per-scenario slices of p5a for the flagship beyond those quoted; the Brev checklist beyond the table, T4 and NVFP4 sections; docs/benchmarks/model-zoo-benchmark.md and docs/reference/models.md (Harbor/LoRA grep hits only; not opened). I fetched NO external data (no HF API calls): every model size/license/gating/date above is the repo's own record of an HF fact taken 2026-09-19 to 2026-09-30 (04's HF facts are 2026-09-19 and predate its 2026-09-23 errata), not [E] for 2026-10-03, and the 'flagship' model id is the P5a spec's claim. This also means the repo contains no look at HF for 2026-era VLMs beyond Qwen3-VL (4B/8B/30B-A3B), Nemotron-Nano-12B-v2-VL, Mistral-Small-3.2-24B, Cosmos-Reason2-8B and Cosmos3-Edge-4B - everything else on HF is unevaluated here.
